target_link_libraries(cahn_hilliard m)
#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

// 3D index calculation
inline constexpr size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z, const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {
    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;
}

// Compute Laplacian with clamped boundary conditions
double computeLaplacian(const std::vector<double>& c, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
                        const double dx, const double dy, const double dz, const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z) {
    const size_t xp = (x < nx - 1) ? x + 1 : x;
    const size_t yp = (y < ny - 1) ? y + 1 : y;
    const size_t zp = (z < nz - 1) ? z + 1 : z;
    const size_t xn = (x > 0) ? x - 1 : 0;
    const size_t yn = (y > 0) ? y - 1 : 0;
    const size_t zn = (z > 0) ? z - 1 : 0;
    
    const double cxx = (c[idx3(xp, y, z, nx, ny)] + c[idx3(xn, y, z, nx, ny)] - 
                  2.0 * c[idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny)]) / (dx * dx);
    const double cyy = (c[idx3(x, yp, z, nx, ny)] + c[idx3(x, yn, z, nx, ny)] - 
                  2.0 * c[idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny)]) / (dy * dy);
    const double czz = (c[idx3(x, y, zp, nx, ny)] + c[idx3(x, y, zn, nx, ny)] - 
                  2.0 * c[idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny)]) / (dz * dz);
    
    return cxx + cyy + czz;
}

// Compute chemical potential
void computeChemicalPotential(const std::vector<double>& c, std::vector<double>& mu,
                              const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
                              const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,
                              const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB) {
    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
                const double cv = c[idx];
                
                mu[idx] = 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB) 
                         + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv
                         - gamma * computeLaplacian(c, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, x, y, z);
            }
        }
    }
}

// Cahn-Hilliard update step
void cahnHilliardUpdate(std::vector<double>& cnew, const std::vector<double>& cold,
                        const std::vector<double>& mu,
                        const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
                        const double D, const double dt, const double dx, const double dy, const double dz) {
    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
                cnew[idx] = cold[idx] + dt * D * 
                           computeLaplacian(mu, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, x, y, z);
            }
        }
    }
}

// Initialize concentration field
void initializeConcentration(std::vector<double>& c, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {
    const size_t vol = nx * ny * nz;
    
    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
                // Generate pseudo-random value in [-1, 1]
                const size_t linear_id = z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;
                const double pseudo = ((((linear_id + 1) * 1299709) % vol) / static_cast<double>(vol));
                c[idx] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;
            }
        }
    }
}

bool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& c, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {
    // Check for NaN or Inf
    for (const auto& val : c) {
        if (std::isnan(val) || std::isinf(val)) {
            printf("Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\n");
            return false;
        }
    }
    
    // Check if values are in reasonable range for concentration field
    // After Cahn-Hilliard evolution, values should typically remain bounded
    double minVal = c[0];
    double maxVal = c[0];
    for (const auto& val : c) {
        minVal = std::min(minVal, val);
        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, val);
    }
    
    printf("Concentration range: [%.6f, %.6f]\n", minVal, maxVal);
    
    // Values should generally stay within reasonable bounds
    if (maxVal > 10.0 || minVal < -10.0) {
        printf("Validation failed: values out of expected range\n");
        return false;
    }
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 64)\n");
    printf("  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\n");
    printf("  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\n");
    printf("  -i <num>     Number of time steps (default: 20)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t nx = 64;
    size_t ny = 0;
    size_t nz = 0;
    int iterations = 20;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-x") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            nx = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-y") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            ny = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-z") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            nz = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-i") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            iterations = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    if (ny == 0) ny = nx;
    if (nz == 0) nz = nx;
    
    printf("Cahn-Hilliard Phase Separation Benchmark\n");
    printf("Grid size: %zu x %zu x %zu\n", nx, ny, nz);
    printf("Time steps: %d\n", iterations);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Physical parameters
    const double dx = 1.0;
    const double dy = 1.0;
    const double dz = 1.0;
    const double dt = 0.01;
    const double e_AA = -(2.0 / 9.0);
    const double e_BB = -(2.0 / 9.0);
    const double e_AB = (2.0 / 9.0);
    const double gamma = 0.5;
    const double D = 1.0;
    
    size_t gridSize = nx * ny * nz;
    
    // Allocate arrays
    std::vector<double> cold(gridSize);
    std::vector<double> cnew(gridSize);
    std::vector<double> mu(gridSize);
    
    // Initialize concentration field
    printf("Initializing concentration field...\n");
    initializeConcentration(cold, nx, ny, nz);
    
    // Run simulation
    printf("Running Cahn-Hilliard simulation...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    for (int t = 0; t < iterations; ++t) {
        // Compute chemical potential
        computeChemicalPotential(cold, mu, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, 
                                gamma, e_AA, e_BB, e_AB);
        
        // Update concentration
        cahnHilliardUpdate(cnew, cold, mu, nx, ny, nz, D, dt, dx, dy, dz);
        
        // Swap buffers
        std::swap(cold, cnew);
    }
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Calculate performance
    double cellUpdates = (double)gridSize * iterations;
    double mcups = cellUpdates / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e6;
    printf("Performance: %.3f MCellUpdates/s\n", mcups);
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        print_results(cold, "Concentration");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateResult(cold, nx, ny, nz);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

#pragma once

#include <cstdint>

// FNV-1a hash, 64 bit length
class hash {
  public:
	using digest = uint64_t;

	template <typename T>
	void add(const T& value) {
		const unsigned char* bytes = reinterpret_cast<const unsigned char*>(&value);
		for(size_t i = 0; i < sizeof(T); ++i) {
			d = (d ^ bytes[i]) * 0x100000001b3ull;
		}
	}

	digest get() const { return d; }

  private:
	digest d = 0xcbf29ce484222325ull;
};
#pragma once

#include <algorithm>
#include <cinttypes>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>

#include "hash.hpp"

// Kahan summation for numerically stable sum
template <typename T>
T kahan_sum(const std::vector<T>& data) {
    T sum = 0.0;
    T c = 0.0;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        T y = val - c;
        T t = sum + y;
        c = (t - sum) - y;
        sum = t;
    }
    return sum;
}

// Print results for floating-point arrays
template <typename T>
void print_results(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute statistics
    T sum = kahan_sum(data);
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Sum: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(sum));
    printf("Min: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %.17e\n", indices[i], static_cast<double>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

// Print results for integer arrays (uses exact hash comparison)
template <typename T>
void print_results_int(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Compute min/max
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Min: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %lld\n", indices[i], static_cast<long long>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

// 3D index calculation
inline constexpr size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z, const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {
    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;
}

// Compute Laplacian with clamped boundary conditions
double computeLaplacian(const std::vector<double>& c, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
                        const double dx, const double dy, const double dz, const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z) {
    const size_t xp = (x < nx - 1) ? x + 1 : x;
    const size_t yp = (y < ny - 1) ? y + 1 : y;
    const size_t zp = (z < nz - 1) ? z + 1 : z;
    const size_t xn = (x > 0) ? x - 1 : 0;
    const size_t yn = (y > 0) ? y - 1 : 0;
    const size_t zn = (z > 0) ? z - 1 : 0;
    
    const double cxx = (c[idx3(xp, y, z, nx, ny)] + c[idx3(xn, y, z, nx, ny)] - 
                  2.0 * c[idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny)]) / (dx * dx);
    const double cyy = (c[idx3(x, yp, z, nx, ny)] + c[idx3(x, yn, z, nx, ny)] - 
                  2.0 * c[idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny)]) / (dy * dy);
    const double czz = (c[idx3(x, y, zp, nx, ny)] + c[idx3(x, y, zn, nx, ny)] - 
                  2.0 * c[idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny)]) / (dz * dz);
    
    return cxx + cyy + czz;
}

// Compute chemical potential
void computeChemicalPotential(const std::vector<double>& c, std::vector<double>& mu,
                              const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
                              const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,
                              const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB) {
    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
                const double cv = c[idx];
                
                mu[idx] = 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB) 
                         + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv
                         - gamma * computeLaplacian(c, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, x, y, z);
            }
        }
    }
}

// Cahn-Hilliard update step
void cahnHilliardUpdate(std::vector<double>& cnew, const std::vector<double>& cold,
                        const std::vector<double>& mu,
                        const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
                        const double D, const double dt, const double dx, const double dy, const double dz) {
    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
                cnew[idx] = cold[idx] + dt * D * 
                           computeLaplacian(mu, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, x, y, z);
            }
        }
    }
}

// Initialize concentration field
void initializeConcentration(std::vector<double>& c, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {
    const size_t vol = nx * ny * nz;
    
    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
                // Generate pseudo-random value in [-1, 1]
                const size_t linear_id = z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;
                const double pseudo = ((((linear_id + 1) * 1299709) % vol) / static_cast<double>(vol));
                c[idx] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;
            }
        }
    }
}

bool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& c, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {
    // Check for NaN or Inf
    for (const auto& val : c) {
        if (std::isnan(val) || std::isinf(val)) {
            printf("Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\n");
            return false;
        }
    }
    
    // Check if values are in reasonable range for concentration field
    // After Cahn-Hilliard evolution, values should typically remain bounded
    double minVal = c[0];
    double maxVal = c[0];
    for (const auto& val : c) {
        minVal = std::min(minVal, val);
        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, val);
    }
    
    printf("Concentration range: [%.6f, %.6f]\n", minVal, maxVal);
    
    // Values should generally stay within reasonable bounds
    if (maxVal > 10.0 || minVal < -10.0) {
        printf("Validation failed: values out of expected range\n");
        return false;
    }
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 64)\n");
    printf("  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\n");
    printf("  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\n");
    printf("  -i <num>     Number of time steps (default: 20)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t nx = 64;
    size_t ny = 0;
    size_t nz = 0;
    int iterations = 20;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-x") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            nx = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-y") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            ny = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-z") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            nz = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-i") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            iterations = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    if (ny == 0) ny = nx;
    if (nz == 0) nz = nx;
    
    printf("Cahn-Hilliard Phase Separation Benchmark\n");
    printf("Grid size: %zu x %zu x %zu\n", nx, ny, nz);
    printf("Time steps: %d\n", iterations);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Physical parameters
    const double dx = 1.0;
    const double dy = 1.0;
    const double dz = 1.0;
    const double dt = 0.01;
    const double e_AA = -(2.0 / 9.0);
    const double e_BB = -(2.0 / 9.0);
    const double e_AB = (2.0 / 9.0);
    const double gamma = 0.5;
    const double D = 1.0;
    
    size_t gridSize = nx * ny * nz;
    
    // Allocate arrays
    std::vector<double> cold(gridSize);
    std::vector<double> cnew(gridSize);
    std::vector<double> mu(gridSize);
    
    // Initialize concentration field
    printf("Initializing concentration field...\n");
    initializeConcentration(cold, nx, ny, nz);
    
    // Run simulation
    printf("Running Cahn-Hilliard simulation...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    for (int t = 0; t < iterations; ++t) {
        // Compute chemical potential
        computeChemicalPotential(cold, mu, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, 
                                gamma, e_AA, e_BB, e_AB);
        
        // Update concentration
        cahnHilliardUpdate(cnew, cold, mu, nx, ny, nz, D, dt, dx, dy, dz);
        
        // Swap buffers
        std::swap(cold, cnew);
    }
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Calculate performance
    double cellUpdates = (double)gridSize * iterations;
    double mcups = cellUpdates / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e6;
    printf("Performance: %.3f MCellUpdates/s\n", mcups);
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        print_results(cold, "Concentration");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateResult(cold, nx, ny, nz);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

#pragma once

#include <cstdint>

// FNV-1a hash, 64 bit length
class hash {
  public:
	using digest = uint64_t;

	template <typename T>
	void add(const T& value) {
		const unsigned char* bytes = reinterpret_cast<const unsigned char*>(&value);
		for(size_t i = 0; i < sizeof(T); ++i) {
			d = (d ^ bytes[i]) * 0x100000001b3ull;
		}
	}

	digest get() const { return d; }

  private:
	digest d = 0xcbf29ce484222325ull;
};
#pragma once

#include <algorithm>
#include <cinttypes>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>

#include "hash.hpp"

// Kahan summation for numerically stable sum
template <typename T>
T kahan_sum(const std::vector<T>& data) {
    T sum = 0.0;
    T c = 0.0;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        T y = val - c;
        T t = sum + y;
        c = (t - sum) - y;
        sum = t;
    }
    return sum;
}

// Print results for floating-point arrays
template <typename T>
void print_results(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute statistics
    T sum = kahan_sum(data);
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Sum: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(sum));
    printf("Min: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %.17e\n", indices[i], static_cast<double>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

// Print results for integer arrays (uses exact hash comparison)
template <typename T>
void print_results_int(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);

        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");

        h.add(val);
    }

    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];

        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);

}

#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <memory>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

#ifndef _OPENMP
#error "This benchmark requires OpenMP support."
#endif

// Row pointers keep boundary handling and index arithmetic out of the SIMD loop.
inline double computeLaplacian(const double* row, const double* yn, const double* yp,
                              const double* zn, const double* zp,
                              const size_t x, const size_t xn, const size_t xp,
                              const double dx2, const double dy2, const double dz2) noexcept {
    const double cv = row[x];
    const double cxx = (row[xp] + row[xn] - 2.0 * cv) / dx2;
    const double cyy = (yp[x] + yn[x] - 2.0 * cv) / dy2;
    const double czz = (zp[x] + zn[x] - 2.0 * cv) / dz2;
    return cxx + cyy + czz;
}

// Called collectively by the persistent OpenMP team. Each thread owns contiguous
// rows; the implicit barrier makes the entire output available to the next sweep.
template <typename CellOperation>
void stencilSweep(const double* __restrict__ field, double* __restrict__ result,
                  const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
                  const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,
                  CellOperation operation) {
    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
    const double dx2 = dx * dx;
    const double dy2 = dy * dy;
    const double dz2 = dz * dz;

    #pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)
    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
            if (nx == 0) continue;
            const size_t offset = z * plane + y * nx;
            const double* row = field + offset;
            const double* yn = row - (y > 0 ? nx : 0);
            const double* yp = row + (y + 1 < ny ? nx : 0);
            const double* zn = row - (z > 0 ? plane : 0);
            const double* zp = row + (z + 1 < nz ? plane : 0);

            const auto apply = [&](const size_t x, const size_t xn, const size_t xp) {
                return operation(offset + x, row[x],
                                 computeLaplacian(row, yn, yp, zn, zp, x, xn, xp, dx2, dy2, dz2));
            };

            result[offset] = apply(0, 0, nx > 1 ? 1 : 0);
            #pragma omp simd
            for (size_t x = 1; x < nx - 1; ++x) {
                result[offset + x] = apply(x, x - 1, x + 1);
            }
            if (nx > 1) {
                result[offset + nx - 1] = apply(nx - 1, nx - 2, nx - 1);
            }
        }
    }
}

void computeChemicalPotential(const double* c, double* mu,
                              const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
                              const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,
                              const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB) {
    stencilSweep(c, mu, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz,
                 [=](size_t, const double cv, const double laplacian) {
                     return 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB)
                            + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv - gamma * laplacian;
                 });
}

// Cahn-Hilliard update step
void cahnHilliardUpdate(double* cnew, const double* cold, const double* mu,
                        const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
                        const double D, const double dt, const double dx, const double dy, const double dz) {
    stencilSweep(mu, cnew, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz,
                 [=](const size_t idx, double, const double laplacian) {
                     return cold[idx] + dt * D * laplacian;
                 });
}

// Initialize concentration field
void initializeConcentration(double* c, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {
    const size_t vol = nx * ny * nz;

    #pragma omp parallel for collapse(2) schedule(static)
    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
                const size_t idx = z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;
                // Generate pseudo-random value in [-1, 1]
                const double pseudo = ((((idx + 1) * 1299709) % vol) / static_cast<double>(vol));
                c[idx] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;
            }
        }
    }
}

bool validateResult(const double* c, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {
    const size_t vol = nx * ny * nz;
    double minVal = c[0];
    double maxVal = c[0];
    int invalid = 0;
    #pragma omp parallel for simd schedule(static) reduction(min:minVal) reduction(max:maxVal) reduction(|:invalid)
    for (size_t idx = 0; idx < vol; ++idx) {
        const double val = c[idx];
        invalid |= std::isnan(val) || std::isinf(val);
        minVal = std::min(minVal, val);
        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, val);
    }

    if (invalid) {
        printf("Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\n");
        return false;
    }

    printf("Concentration range: [%.6f, %.6f]\n", minVal, maxVal);
    
    // Values should generally stay within reasonable bounds
    if (maxVal > 10.0 || minVal < -10.0) {
        printf("Validation failed: values out of expected range\n");
        return false;
    }
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 64)\n");
    printf("  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\n");
    printf("  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\n");
    printf("  -i <num>     Number of time steps (default: 20)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t nx = 64;
    size_t ny = 0;
    size_t nz = 0;
    int iterations = 20;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-x") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            nx = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-y") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            ny = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-z") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            nz = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-i") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            iterations = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    if (ny == 0) ny = nx;
    if (nz == 0) nz = nx;
    
    printf("Cahn-Hilliard Phase Separation Benchmark\n");
    printf("Grid size: %zu x %zu x %zu\n", nx, ny, nz);
    printf("Time steps: %d\n", iterations);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Physical parameters
    const double dx = 1.0;
    const double dy = 1.0;
    const double dz = 1.0;
    const double dt = 0.01;
    const double e_AA = -(2.0 / 9.0);
    const double e_BB = -(2.0 / 9.0);
    const double e_AB = (2.0 / 9.0);
    const double gamma = 0.5;
    const double D = 1.0;
    
    size_t gridSize = nx * ny * nz;
    
    // Leave storage uninitialized so the threads that use each partition also
    // first-touch its pages. Both scratch arrays are fully written before use.
    auto cold = std::make_unique_for_overwrite<double[]>(gridSize);
    auto cnew = std::make_unique_for_overwrite<double[]>(gridSize);
    auto mu = std::make_unique_for_overwrite<double[]>(gridSize);
    
    // Initialize concentration field
    printf("Initializing concentration field...\n");
    initializeConcentration(cold.get(), nx, ny, nz);
    
    // Run simulation
    printf("Running Cahn-Hilliard simulation...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    #pragma omp parallel
    {
        double* current = cold.get();
        double* next = cnew.get();
        double* potential = mu.get();
        for (int t = 0; t < iterations; ++t) {
            computeChemicalPotential(current, potential, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz,
                                     gamma, e_AA, e_BB, e_AB);
            cahnHilliardUpdate(next, current, potential, nx, ny, nz, D, dt, dx, dy, dz);

            // Each thread swaps its own pointers after the update barrier. This
            // needs no single region or additional synchronization.
            std::swap(current, next);
        }
    }
    if (iterations > 0 && iterations % 2 != 0) {
        std::swap(cold, cnew);
    }
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Calculate performance
    double cellUpdates = (double)gridSize * iterations;
    double mcups = cellUpdates / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e6;
    printf("Performance: %.3f MCellUpdates/s\n", mcups);
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        print_results(std::vector<double>(cold.get(), cold.get() + gridSize), "Concentration");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateResult(cold.get(), nx, ny, nz);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

+// Called collectively by the persistent OpenMP team. Each thread owns contiguous

+template <typename CellOperation>
+void stencilSweep(const double* __restrict__ field, double* __restrict__ result,
+                  const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
+                  const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,
+                  CellOperation operation) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const double dx2 = dx * dx;
+    const double dy2 = dy * dy;
+    const double dz2 = dz * dz;

-                         + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv
-                         - gamma * computeLaplacian(c, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, x, y, z);

+            const double* yn = row - (y > 0 ? nx : 0);
+            const double* yp = row + (y + 1 < ny ? nx : 0);
+            const double* zn = row - (z > 0 ? plane : 0);
+            const double* zp = row + (z + 1 < nz ? plane : 0);

             }

         }

+// Called collectively by the persistent OpenMP team. Each thread owns contiguous

+template <typename CellOperation>
+void stencilSweep(const double* __restrict__ field, double* __restrict__ result,
+                  const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
+                  const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,
+                  CellOperation operation) {

+    const double dy2 = dy * dy;
+    const double dz2 = dz * dz;

The first implementation matches the original exactly in all tested result fields, including hashes, across 1, 2, 4, and 8 threads. The tests cover single-cell dimensions, uneven grids, and odd/even step counts. Both stencil loops also vectorize successfully; I’m now measuring scaling on larger grids.

+// Row pointers keep boundary handling and index arithmetic out of the SIMD loop.
+inline double computeLaplacian(const double* row, const double* yn, const double* yp,
+                              const double* zn, const double* zp,
+                              const size_t x, const size_t xn, const size_t xp,
+                              const double dx2, const double dy2, const double dz2) noexcept {
+    const double cv = row[x];
+    const double cxx = (row[xp] + row[xn] - 2.0 * cv) / dx2;
+    const double cyy = (yp[x] + yn[x] - 2.0 * cv) / dy2;

-                              const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB) {
+// Called collectively by the persistent OpenMP team. Each thread owns contiguous
+// rows; the implicit barrier makes the entire output available to the next sweep.
+template <typename CellOperation>
+void stencilSweep(const double* __restrict__ field, double* __restrict__ result,
+                  const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
+                  const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,
+                  CellOperation operation) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const double dx2 = dx * dx;
+    const double dy2 = dy * dy;
+    const double dz2 = dz * dz;
+
+    #pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)
     for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
         for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
-            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
-                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
-                const double cv = c[idx];
-                
-                mu[idx] = 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB) 
-                         + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv
-                         - gamma * computeLaplacian(c, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, x, y, z);
+            if (nx == 0) continue;
+            const size_t offset = z * plane + y * nx;
+            const double* row = field + offset;
+            const double* yn = row - (y > 0 ? nx : 0);
+            const double* yp = row + (y + 1 < ny ? nx : 0);
+            const double* zn = row - (z > 0 ? plane : 0);
+            const double* zp = row + (z + 1 < nz ? plane : 0);
+
+            const auto apply = [&](const size_t x, const size_t xn, const size_t xp) {
+                return operation(offset + x, row[x],
+                                 computeLaplacian(row, yn, yp, zn, zp, x, xn, xp, dx2, dy2, dz2));
+            };
+
+            result[offset] = apply(0, 0, nx > 1 ? 1 : 0);
+            #pragma omp simd
+            for (size_t x = 1; x < nx - 1; ++x) {
+                result[offset + x] = apply(x, x - 1, x + 1);
             }
+            if (nx > 1) {
+                result[offset + nx - 1] = apply(nx - 1, nx - 2, nx - 1);
+            }
         }
     }
 }
 
+void computeChemicalPotential(const double* c, double* mu,
+                              const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
+                              const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,
+                              const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB) {
+    stencilSweep(c, mu, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz,
+                 [=](size_t, const double cv, const double laplacian) {
+                     return 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB)
+                            + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv - gamma * laplacian;
+                 });

The row-based version scales well on grids that fit in cache, but larger grids become limited by memory bandwidth. I’ll test processing tiles whose chemical-potential halos fit in each thread’s cache. That could reduce memory traffic and remove one barrier per step while preserving the calculation.

/bin/bash -lc 'cat cahn-hilliard/cahn_hilliard.cpp' in /home/llmtest/evals/20261006-204623/cahn-hilliard_gpt-6.1-sol-xhigh_omp_r4
 succeeded in 0ms:
#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <memory>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

#ifndef _OPENMP
#error "This benchmark requires OpenMP support."
#endif

// Row pointers keep boundary handling and index arithmetic out of the SIMD loop.
inline double computeLaplacian(const double* row, const double* yn, const double* yp,
                              const double* zn, const double* zp,
                              const size_t x, const size_t xn, const size_t xp,
                              const double dx2, const double dy2, const double dz2) noexcept {
    const double cv = row[x];
    const double cxx = (row[xp] + row[xn] - 2.0 * cv) / dx2;
    const double cyy = (yp[x] + yn[x] - 2.0 * cv) / dy2;
    const double czz = (zp[x] + zn[x] - 2.0 * cv) / dz2;
    return cxx + cyy + czz;
}

// Called collectively by the persistent OpenMP team. Each thread owns contiguous
// rows; the implicit barrier makes the entire output available to the next sweep.
template <typename CellOperation>
void stencilSweep(const double* __restrict__ field, double* __restrict__ result,
                  const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
                  const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,
                  CellOperation operation) {
    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
    const double dx2 = dx * dx;
    const double dy2 = dy * dy;
    const double dz2 = dz * dz;

    #pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)
    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
            if (nx == 0) continue;
            const size_t offset = z * plane + y * nx;
            const double* row = field + offset;
            const double* yn = row - (y > 0 ? nx : 0);
            const double* yp = row + (y + 1 < ny ? nx : 0);
            const double* zn = row - (z > 0 ? plane : 0);
            const double* zp = row + (z + 1 < nz ? plane : 0);

            const auto apply = [&](const size_t x, const size_t xn, const size_t xp) {
                return operation(offset + x, row[x],
                                 computeLaplacian(row, yn, yp, zn, zp, x, xn, xp, dx2, dy2, dz2));
            };

            result[offset] = apply(0, 0, nx > 1 ? 1 : 0);
            #pragma omp simd
            for (size_t x = 1; x < nx - 1; ++x) {
                result[offset + x] = apply(x, x - 1, x + 1);
            }
            if (nx > 1) {
                result[offset + nx - 1] = apply(nx - 1, nx - 2, nx - 1);
            }
        }
    }
}

void computeChemicalPotential(const double* c, double* mu,
                              const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
                              const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,
                              const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB) {
    stencilSweep(c, mu, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz,
                 [=](size_t, const double cv, const double laplacian) {
                     return 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB)
                            + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv - gamma * laplacian;
                 });
}

// Cahn-Hilliard update step
void cahnHilliardUpdate(double* cnew, const double* cold, const double* mu,
                        const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
                        const double D, const double dt, const double dx, const double dy, const double dz) {
    stencilSweep(mu, cnew, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz,
                 [=](const size_t idx, double, const double laplacian) {
                     return cold[idx] + dt * D * laplacian;
                 });
}

// Initialize concentration field
void initializeConcentration(double* c, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {
    const size_t vol = nx * ny * nz;

    #pragma omp parallel for collapse(2) schedule(static)
    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
                const size_t idx = z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;
                // Generate pseudo-random value in [-1, 1]
                const double pseudo = ((((idx + 1) * 1299709) % vol) / static_cast<double>(vol));
                c[idx] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;
            }
        }
    }
}

bool validateResult(const double* c, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {
    const size_t vol = nx * ny * nz;
    double minVal = c[0];
    double maxVal = c[0];
    int invalid = 0;
    #pragma omp parallel for simd schedule(static) reduction(min:minVal) reduction(max:maxVal) reduction(|:invalid)
    for (size_t idx = 0; idx < vol; ++idx) {
        const double val = c[idx];
        invalid |= std::isnan(val) || std::isinf(val);
        minVal = std::min(minVal, val);
        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, val);
    }

    if (invalid) {
        printf("Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\n");
        return false;
    }

    printf("Concentration range: [%.6f, %.6f]\n", minVal, maxVal);
    
    // Values should generally stay within reasonable bounds
    if (maxVal > 10.0 || minVal < -10.0) {
        printf("Validation failed: values out of expected range\n");
        return false;
    }
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 64)\n");
    printf("  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\n");
    printf("  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\n");
    printf("  -i <num>     Number of time steps (default: 20)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t nx = 64;
    size_t ny = 0;
    size_t nz = 0;
    int iterations = 20;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-x") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            nx = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-y") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            ny = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-z") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            nz = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-i") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            iterations = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    if (ny == 0) ny = nx;
    if (nz == 0) nz = nx;
    
    printf("Cahn-Hilliard Phase Separation Benchmark\n");
    printf("Grid size: %zu x %zu x %zu\n", nx, ny, nz);
    printf("Time steps: %d\n", iterations);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Physical parameters
    const double dx = 1.0;
    const double dy = 1.0;
    const double dz = 1.0;
    const double dt = 0.01;
    const double e_AA = -(2.0 / 9.0);
    const double e_BB = -(2.0 / 9.0);
    const double e_AB = (2.0 / 9.0);
    const double gamma = 0.5;
    const double D = 1.0;
    
    size_t gridSize = nx * ny * nz;
    
    // Leave storage uninitialized so the threads that use each partition also
    // first-touch its pages. Both scratch arrays are fully written before use.
    auto cold = std::make_unique_for_overwrite<double[]>(gridSize);
    auto cnew = std::make_unique_for_overwrite<double[]>(gridSize);
    auto mu = std::make_unique_for_overwrite<double[]>(gridSize);
    
    // Initialize concentration field
    printf("Initializing concentration field...\n");
    initializeConcentration(cold.get(), nx, ny, nz);
    
    // Run simulation
    printf("Running Cahn-Hilliard simulation...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    #pragma omp parallel
    {
        double* current = cold.get();
        double* next = cnew.get();
        double* potential = mu.get();
        for (int t = 0; t < iterations; ++t) {
            computeChemicalPotential(current, potential, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz,
                                     gamma, e_AA, e_BB, e_AB);
            cahnHilliardUpdate(next, current, potential, nx, ny, nz, D, dt, dx, dy, dz);

            // Each thread swaps its own pointers after the update barrier. This
            // needs no single region or additional synchronization.
            std::swap(current, next);
        }
    }
    if (iterations > 0 && iterations % 2 != 0) {
        std::swap(cold, cnew);
    }
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Calculate performance
    double cellUpdates = (double)gridSize * iterations;
    double mcups = cellUpdates / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e6;
    printf("Performance: %.3f MCellUpdates/s\n", mcups);
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        print_results(std::vector<double>(cold.get(), cold.get() + gridSize), "Concentration");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateResult(cold.get(), nx, ny, nz);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

+inline double computeLaplacian(const double* row, const double* yn, const double* yp,

+// The potential tile, its concentration data and its output fit in a private
+// CPU cache. Halos contain real neighboring cells, with clamping only at the
+// physical domain boundary.
+constexpr size_t tileX = 128;
+constexpr size_t tileY = 8;
+constexpr size_t tileZ = 8;
+
+// Apply a stencil to part of a row. Only physical X boundaries need scalar
+// handling; both tile interiors and their halos use contiguous SIMD loads.
+template <typename CellOperation>
+void stencilRow(const double* row, const double* yn, const double* yp,
+                const double* zn, const double* zp, double* __restrict__ result,
+                const size_t xBegin, const size_t xEnd, const size_t nx,
+                const size_t fieldXBegin,
+                const double dx2, const double dy2, const double dz2,
+                CellOperation operation) {
+    const auto apply = [&](const size_t x, const size_t xn, const size_t xp) {
+        const size_t localX = x - fieldXBegin;
+        return operation(x, row[localX],
+                         computeLaplacian(row, yn, yp, zn, zp, localX,
+                                          xn - fieldXBegin, xp - fieldXBegin, dx2, dy2, dz2));
+    };
+
+    const size_t interiorBegin = std::max(xBegin, size_t{1});
+    const size_t interiorEnd = std::min(xEnd, nx - 1);
+    if (xBegin == 0) {
+        result[0] = apply(0, 0, nx > 1 ? 1 : 0);
+    }
+    #pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t x = interiorBegin; x < interiorEnd; ++x) {
+        result[x - xBegin] = apply(x, x - 1, x + 1);
     }
+    if (xEnd == nx && nx > 1) {
+        result[nx - 1 - xBegin] = apply(nx - 1, nx - 2, nx - 1);
+    }
 }

+// Compute a tile's chemical potential, including a one-cell halo, directly from
+// the immutable old concentration. Then update just the tile's owned cells.
+// Potentials are thread-private, so tiles need no intermediate global barrier.
+void advanceTile(const double* __restrict__ cold, double* __restrict__ cnew,
+                 double* __restrict__ mu,
+                 const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
+                 const size_t xBegin, const size_t xEnd,
+                 const size_t yBegin, const size_t yEnd,
+                 const size_t zBegin, const size_t zEnd,
+                 const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,
+                 const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB,
+                 const double D, const double dt) {
+    const size_t hxBegin = xBegin > 0 ? xBegin - 1 : 0;
+    const size_t hxEnd = xEnd < nx ? xEnd + 1 : nx;
+    const size_t hyBegin = yBegin > 0 ? yBegin - 1 : 0;
+    const size_t hyEnd = yEnd < ny ? yEnd + 1 : ny;
+    const size_t hzBegin = zBegin > 0 ? zBegin - 1 : 0;
+    const size_t hzEnd = zEnd < nz ? zEnd + 1 : nz;
+    const size_t muStride = hxEnd - hxBegin;
+    const size_t muPlane = muStride * (hyEnd - hyBegin);
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const double dx2 = dx * dx;
+    const double dy2 = dy * dy;
+    const double dz2 = dz * dz;
+
+    for (size_t z = hzBegin; z < hzEnd; ++z) {
+        for (size_t y = hyBegin; y < hyEnd; ++y) {
+            const double* row = cold + z * plane + y * nx;
+            double* potentialRow = mu + (z - hzBegin) * muPlane + (y - hyBegin) * muStride;
+            stencilRow(row, row - (y > 0 ? nx : 0), row + (y + 1 < ny ? nx : 0),
+                       row - (z > 0 ? plane : 0), row + (z + 1 < nz ? plane : 0),
+                       potentialRow, hxBegin, hxEnd, nx, 0, dx2, dy2, dz2,
+                       [=](size_t, const double cv, const double laplacian) {
+                           return 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB)
+                                  + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv - gamma * laplacian;
+                       });
+        }
+    }
+
+    for (size_t z = zBegin; z < zEnd; ++z) {
+        for (size_t y = yBegin; y < yEnd; ++y) {
+            const double* oldRow = cold + z * plane + y * nx;
+            const double* row = mu + (z - hzBegin) * muPlane + (y - hyBegin) * muStride;
+            double* nextRow = cnew + z * plane + y * nx + xBegin;
+            stencilRow(row, row - (y > 0 ? muStride : 0), row + (y + 1 < ny ? muStride : 0),
+                       row - (z > 0 ? muPlane : 0), row + (z + 1 < nz ? muPlane : 0),
+                       nextRow, xBegin, xEnd, nx, hxBegin, dx2, dy2, dz2,
+                       [=](const size_t x, double, const double laplacian) {
+                           return oldRow[x] + dt * D * laplacian;
+                       });
         }
     }
 }

+// Row pointers keep boundary handling and index arithmetic out of the SIMD loop.
+inline double computeLaplacian(const double* row, const double* yn, const double* yp,
+                              const double* zn, const double* zp,
+                              const size_t x, const size_t xn, const size_t xp,
+                              const double dx2, const double dy2, const double dz2) noexcept {
+    const double cv = row[x];
+    const double cxx = (row[xp] + row[xn] - 2.0 * cv) / dx2;
+    const double cyy = (yp[x] + yn[x] - 2.0 * cv) / dy2;

     return cxx + cyy + czz;

-void computeChemicalPotential(const std::vector<double>& c, std::vector<double>& mu,

+// The potential tile, its concentration data and its output fit in a private
+// CPU cache. Halos contain real neighboring cells, with clamping only at the
+// physical domain boundary.
+constexpr size_t tileX = 128;
+constexpr size_t tileY = 8;
+constexpr size_t tileZ = 8;
+
+// Apply a stencil to part of a row. Only physical X boundaries need scalar
+// handling; both tile interiors and their halos use contiguous SIMD loads.
+template <typename CellOperation>
+void stencilRow(const double* row, const double* yn, const double* yp,
+                const double* zn, const double* zp, double* __restrict__ result,
+                const size_t xBegin, const size_t xEnd, const size_t nx,
+                const size_t fieldXBegin,
+                const double dx2, const double dy2, const double dz2,
+                CellOperation operation) {
+    const auto apply = [&](const size_t x, const size_t xn, const size_t xp) {
+        const size_t localX = x - fieldXBegin;
+        return operation(x, row[localX],
+                         computeLaplacian(row, yn, yp, zn, zp, localX,
+                                          xn - fieldXBegin, xp - fieldXBegin, dx2, dy2, dz2));
+    };
+
+    const size_t interiorBegin = std::max(xBegin, size_t{1});
+    const size_t interiorEnd = std::min(xEnd, nx - 1);
+    if (xBegin == 0) {
+        result[0] = apply(0, 0, nx > 1 ? 1 : 0);
+    }
+    #pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t x = interiorBegin; x < interiorEnd; ++x) {
+        result[x - xBegin] = apply(x, x - 1, x + 1);
     }
+    if (xEnd == nx && nx > 1) {
+        result[nx - 1 - xBegin] = apply(nx - 1, nx - 2, nx - 1);
+    }
 }
 
-// Cahn-Hilliard update step
-void cahnHilliardUpdate(std::vector<double>& cnew, const std::vector<double>& cold,
-                        const std::vector<double>& mu,
-                        const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
-                        const double D, const double dt, const double dx, const double dy, const double dz) {
-    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {

-            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
-                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
-                cnew[idx] = cold[idx] + dt * D * 
-                           computeLaplacian(mu, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, x, y, z);
-            }
+// Compute a tile's chemical potential, including a one-cell halo, directly from
+// the immutable old concentration. Then update just the tile's owned cells.
+// Potentials are thread-private, so tiles need no intermediate global barrier.
+void advanceTile(const double* __restrict__ cold, double* __restrict__ cnew,
+                 double* __restrict__ mu,
+                 const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
+                 const size_t xBegin, const size_t xEnd,
+                 const size_t yBegin, const size_t yEnd,
+                 const size_t zBegin, const size_t zEnd,
+                 const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,
+                 const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB,
+                 const double D, const double dt) {
+    const size_t hxBegin = xBegin > 0 ? xBegin - 1 : 0;
+    const size_t hxEnd = xEnd < nx ? xEnd + 1 : nx;
+    const size_t hyBegin = yBegin > 0 ? yBegin - 1 : 0;
+    const size_t hyEnd = yEnd < ny ? yEnd + 1 : ny;
+    const size_t hzBegin = zBegin > 0 ? zBegin - 1 : 0;
+    const size_t hzEnd = zEnd < nz ? zEnd + 1 : nz;
+    const size_t muStride = hxEnd - hxBegin;
+    const size_t muPlane = muStride * (hyEnd - hyBegin);
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const double dx2 = dx * dx;
+    const double dy2 = dy * dy;
+    const double dz2 = dz * dz;
+
+    for (size_t z = hzBegin; z < hzEnd; ++z) {
+        for (size_t y = hyBegin; y < hyEnd; ++y) {
+            const double* row = cold + z * plane + y * nx;
+            double* potentialRow = mu + (z - hzBegin) * muPlane + (y - hyBegin) * muStride;
+            stencilRow(row, row - (y > 0 ? nx : 0), row + (y + 1 < ny ? nx : 0),
+                       row - (z > 0 ? plane : 0), row + (z + 1 < nz ? plane : 0),
+                       potentialRow, hxBegin, hxEnd, nx, 0, dx2, dy2, dz2,
+                       [=](size_t, const double cv, const double laplacian) {
+                           return 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB)
+                                  + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv - gamma * laplacian;
+                       });
+        }
+    }
+
+    for (size_t z = zBegin; z < zEnd; ++z) {
+        for (size_t y = yBegin; y < yEnd; ++y) {
+            const double* oldRow = cold + z * plane + y * nx;
+            const double* row = mu + (z - hzBegin) * muPlane + (y - hyBegin) * muStride;
+            double* nextRow = cnew + z * plane + y * nx + xBegin;
+            stencilRow(row, row - (y > 0 ? muStride : 0), row + (y + 1 < ny ? muStride : 0),
+                       row - (z > 0 ? muPlane : 0), row + (z + 1 < nz ? muPlane : 0),
+                       nextRow, xBegin, xEnd, nx, hxBegin, dx2, dy2, dz2,
+                       [=](const size_t x, double, const double laplacian) {
+                           return oldRow[x] + dt * D * laplacian;
+                       });
         }
     }
 }

+// Row pointers keep boundary handling and index arithmetic out of the SIMD loop.
+inline double computeLaplacian(const double* row, const double* yn, const double* yp,
+                              const double* zn, const double* zp,
+                              const size_t x, const size_t xn, const size_t xp,

+// The potential tile, its concentration data and its output fit in a private
+// CPU cache. Halos contain real neighboring cells, with clamping only at the
+// physical domain boundary.
+constexpr size_t tileX = 128;
+constexpr size_t tileY = 8;
+constexpr size_t tileZ = 8;
+
+// Apply a stencil to part of a row. Only physical X boundaries need scalar
+// handling; both tile interiors and their halos use contiguous SIMD loads.
+template <typename CellOperation>
+void stencilRow(const double* row, const double* yn, const double* yp,
+                const double* zn, const double* zp, double* __restrict__ result,
+                const size_t xBegin, const size_t xEnd, const size_t nx,
+                const size_t fieldXBegin,
+                const double dx2, const double dy2, const double dz2,
+                CellOperation operation) {
+    const auto apply = [&](const size_t x, const size_t xn, const size_t xp) {
+        const size_t localX = x - fieldXBegin;
+        return operation(x, row[localX],
+                         computeLaplacian(row, yn, yp, zn, zp, localX,
+                                          xn - fieldXBegin, xp - fieldXBegin, dx2, dy2, dz2));
+    };
+
+    const size_t interiorBegin = std::max(xBegin, size_t{1});
+    const size_t interiorEnd = std::min(xEnd, nx - 1);
+    if (xBegin == 0) {
+        result[0] = apply(0, 0, nx > 1 ? 1 : 0);
+    }
+    #pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t x = interiorBegin; x < interiorEnd; ++x) {
+        result[x - xBegin] = apply(x, x - 1, x + 1);
     }
+    if (xEnd == nx && nx > 1) {
+        result[nx - 1 - xBegin] = apply(nx - 1, nx - 2, nx - 1);
+    }
 }
 
-// Cahn-Hilliard update step
-void cahnHilliardUpdate(std::vector<double>& cnew, const std::vector<double>& cold,

-                        const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
-                        const double D, const double dt, const double dx, const double dy, const double dz) {

-                cnew[idx] = cold[idx] + dt * D * 
-                           computeLaplacian(mu, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, x, y, z);
-            }
+// Compute a tile's chemical potential, including a one-cell halo, directly from
+// the immutable old concentration. Then update just the tile's owned cells.
+// Potentials are thread-private, so tiles need no intermediate global barrier.
+void advanceTile(const double* __restrict__ cold, double* __restrict__ cnew,
+                 double* __restrict__ mu,
+                 const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
+                 const size_t xBegin, const size_t xEnd,
+                 const size_t yBegin, const size_t yEnd,
+                 const size_t zBegin, const size_t zEnd,
+                 const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,
+                 const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB,
+                 const double D, const double dt) {
+    const size_t hxBegin = xBegin > 0 ? xBegin - 1 : 0;
+    const size_t hxEnd = xEnd < nx ? xEnd + 1 : nx;
+    const size_t hyBegin = yBegin > 0 ? yBegin - 1 : 0;
+    const size_t hyEnd = yEnd < ny ? yEnd + 1 : ny;
+    const size_t hzBegin = zBegin > 0 ? zBegin - 1 : 0;
+    const size_t hzEnd = zEnd < nz ? zEnd + 1 : nz;
+    const size_t muStride = hxEnd - hxBegin;
+    const size_t muPlane = muStride * (hyEnd - hyBegin);
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const double dx2 = dx * dx;
+    const double dy2 = dy * dy;
+    const double dz2 = dz * dz;
+
+    for (size_t z = hzBegin; z < hzEnd; ++z) {
+        for (size_t y = hyBegin; y < hyEnd; ++y) {
+            const double* row = cold + z * plane + y * nx;
+            double* potentialRow = mu + (z - hzBegin) * muPlane + (y - hyBegin) * muStride;
+            stencilRow(row, row - (y > 0 ? nx : 0), row + (y + 1 < ny ? nx : 0),
+                       row - (z > 0 ? plane : 0), row + (z + 1 < nz ? plane : 0),
+                       potentialRow, hxBegin, hxEnd, nx, 0, dx2, dy2, dz2,
+                       [=](size_t, const double cv, const double laplacian) {
+                           return 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB)
+                                  + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv - gamma * laplacian;
+                       });
+        }
+    }
+
+    for (size_t z = zBegin; z < zEnd; ++z) {
+        for (size_t y = yBegin; y < yEnd; ++y) {
+            const double* oldRow = cold + z * plane + y * nx;
+            const double* row = mu + (z - hzBegin) * muPlane + (y - hyBegin) * muStride;
+            double* nextRow = cnew + z * plane + y * nx + xBegin;
+            stencilRow(row, row - (y > 0 ? muStride : 0), row + (y + 1 < ny ? muStride : 0),
+                       row - (z > 0 ? muPlane : 0), row + (z + 1 < nz ? muPlane : 0),
+                       nextRow, xBegin, xEnd, nx, hxBegin, dx2, dy2, dz2,
+                       [=](const size_t x, double, const double laplacian) {
+                           return oldRow[x] + dt * D * laplacian;
+                       });
         }
     }
 }

+                                          xn - fieldXBegin, xp - fieldXBegin, dx2, dy2, dz2));
+    };
+
+    const size_t interiorBegin = std::max(xBegin, size_t{1});
+    const size_t interiorEnd = std::min(xEnd, nx - 1);
+    if (xBegin == 0) {
+        result[0] = apply(0, 0, nx > 1 ? 1 : 0);
+    }
+    #pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t x = interiorBegin; x < interiorEnd; ++x) {
+        result[x - xBegin] = apply(x, x - 1, x + 1);
     }
+    if (xEnd == nx && nx > 1) {
+        result[nx - 1 - xBegin] = apply(nx - 1, nx - 2, nx - 1);
+    }

-// Cahn-Hilliard update step
-void cahnHilliardUpdate(std::vector<double>& cnew, const std::vector<double>& cold,
-                        const std::vector<double>& mu,
-                        const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
-                        const double D, const double dt, const double dx, const double dy, const double dz) {
-    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
-        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {

-                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
-                cnew[idx] = cold[idx] + dt * D * 
-                           computeLaplacian(mu, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, x, y, z);
-            }
+// Compute a tile's chemical potential, including a one-cell halo, directly from
+// the immutable old concentration. Then update just the tile's owned cells.
+// Potentials are thread-private, so tiles need no intermediate global barrier.
+void advanceTile(const double* __restrict__ cold, double* __restrict__ cnew,
+                 double* __restrict__ mu,
+                 const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
+                 const size_t xBegin, const size_t xEnd,
+                 const size_t yBegin, const size_t yEnd,
+                 const size_t zBegin, const size_t zEnd,
+                 const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,
+                 const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB,
+                 const double D, const double dt) {
+    const size_t hxBegin = xBegin > 0 ? xBegin - 1 : 0;
+    const size_t hxEnd = xEnd < nx ? xEnd + 1 : nx;
+    const size_t hyBegin = yBegin > 0 ? yBegin - 1 : 0;
+    const size_t hyEnd = yEnd < ny ? yEnd + 1 : ny;
+    const size_t hzBegin = zBegin > 0 ? zBegin - 1 : 0;
+    const size_t hzEnd = zEnd < nz ? zEnd + 1 : nz;
+    const size_t muStride = hxEnd - hxBegin;
+    const size_t muPlane = muStride * (hyEnd - hyBegin);
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const double dx2 = dx * dx;
+    const double dy2 = dy * dy;
+    const double dz2 = dz * dz;
+
+    for (size_t z = hzBegin; z < hzEnd; ++z) {
+        for (size_t y = hyBegin; y < hyEnd; ++y) {
+            const double* row = cold + z * plane + y * nx;
+            double* potentialRow = mu + (z - hzBegin) * muPlane + (y - hyBegin) * muStride;
+            stencilRow(row, row - (y > 0 ? nx : 0), row + (y + 1 < ny ? nx : 0),
+                       row - (z > 0 ? plane : 0), row + (z + 1 < nz ? plane : 0),
+                       potentialRow, hxBegin, hxEnd, nx, 0, dx2, dy2, dz2,
+                       [=](size_t, const double cv, const double laplacian) {
+                           return 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB)
+                                  + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv - gamma * laplacian;
+                       });
+        }
+    }
+
+    for (size_t z = zBegin; z < zEnd; ++z) {
+        for (size_t y = yBegin; y < yEnd; ++y) {
+            const double* oldRow = cold + z * plane + y * nx;
+            const double* row = mu + (z - hzBegin) * muPlane + (y - hyBegin) * muStride;
+            double* nextRow = cnew + z * plane + y * nx + xBegin;
+            stencilRow(row, row - (y > 0 ? muStride : 0), row + (y + 1 < ny ? muStride : 0),
+                       row - (z > 0 ? muPlane : 0), row + (z + 1 < nz ? muPlane : 0),
+                       nextRow, xBegin, xEnd, nx, hxBegin, dx2, dy2, dz2,
+                       [=](const size_t x, double, const double laplacian) {
+                           return oldRow[x] + dt * D * laplacian;
+                       });
         }
     }
 }

+inline double computeLaplacian(const double* row, const double* yn, const double* yp,

+// The potential tile, its concentration data and its output fit in a private
+// CPU cache. Halos contain real neighboring cells, with clamping only at the
+// physical domain boundary.
+constexpr size_t tileX = 128;
+constexpr size_t tileY = 8;
+constexpr size_t tileZ = 8;
+
+// Apply a stencil to part of a row. Only physical X boundaries need scalar
+// handling; both tile interiors and their halos use contiguous SIMD loads.
+template <typename CellOperation>
+void stencilRow(const double* row, const double* yn, const double* yp,
+                const double* zn, const double* zp, double* __restrict__ result,
+                const size_t xBegin, const size_t xEnd, const size_t nx,
+                const size_t fieldXBegin,
+                const double dx2, const double dy2, const double dz2,
+                CellOperation operation) {
+    const auto apply = [&](const size_t x, const size_t xn, const size_t xp) {
+        const size_t localX = x - fieldXBegin;
+        return operation(x, row[localX],
+                         computeLaplacian(row, yn, yp, zn, zp, localX,
+                                          xn - fieldXBegin, xp - fieldXBegin, dx2, dy2, dz2));
+    };
+
+    const size_t interiorBegin = std::max(xBegin, size_t{1});
+    const size_t interiorEnd = std::min(xEnd, nx - 1);
+    if (xBegin == 0) {
+        result[0] = apply(0, 0, nx > 1 ? 1 : 0);
+    }
+    #pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t x = interiorBegin; x < interiorEnd; ++x) {
+        result[x - xBegin] = apply(x, x - 1, x + 1);
     }
+    if (xEnd == nx && nx > 1) {
+        result[nx - 1 - xBegin] = apply(nx - 1, nx - 2, nx - 1);
+    }

+// Compute a tile's chemical potential, including a one-cell halo, directly from
+// the immutable old concentration. Then update just the tile's owned cells.
+// Potentials are thread-private, so tiles need no intermediate global barrier.
+void advanceTile(const double* __restrict__ cold, double* __restrict__ cnew,
+                 double* __restrict__ mu,
+                 const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
+                 const size_t xBegin, const size_t xEnd,
+                 const size_t yBegin, const size_t yEnd,
+                 const size_t zBegin, const size_t zEnd,
+                 const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,
+                 const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB,
+                 const double D, const double dt) {
+    const size_t hxBegin = xBegin > 0 ? xBegin - 1 : 0;
+    const size_t hxEnd = xEnd < nx ? xEnd + 1 : nx;
+    const size_t hyBegin = yBegin > 0 ? yBegin - 1 : 0;
+    const size_t hyEnd = yEnd < ny ? yEnd + 1 : ny;
+    const size_t hzBegin = zBegin > 0 ? zBegin - 1 : 0;
+    const size_t hzEnd = zEnd < nz ? zEnd + 1 : nz;
+    const size_t muStride = hxEnd - hxBegin;
+    const size_t muPlane = muStride * (hyEnd - hyBegin);
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const double dx2 = dx * dx;
+    const double dy2 = dy * dy;
+    const double dz2 = dz * dz;
+
+    for (size_t z = hzBegin; z < hzEnd; ++z) {
+        for (size_t y = hyBegin; y < hyEnd; ++y) {
+            const double* row = cold + z * plane + y * nx;
+            double* potentialRow = mu + (z - hzBegin) * muPlane + (y - hyBegin) * muStride;
+            stencilRow(row, row - (y > 0 ? nx : 0), row + (y + 1 < ny ? nx : 0),
+                       row - (z > 0 ? plane : 0), row + (z + 1 < nz ? plane : 0),
+                       potentialRow, hxBegin, hxEnd, nx, 0, dx2, dy2, dz2,
+                       [=](size_t, const double cv, const double laplacian) {
+                           return 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB)
+                                  + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv - gamma * laplacian;
+                       });
+        }
+    }
+
+    for (size_t z = zBegin; z < zEnd; ++z) {
+        for (size_t y = yBegin; y < yEnd; ++y) {
+            const double* oldRow = cold + z * plane + y * nx;
+            const double* row = mu + (z - hzBegin) * muPlane + (y - hyBegin) * muStride;

+                       nextRow, xBegin, xEnd, nx, hxBegin, dx2, dy2, dz2,

+                       });

+// Row pointers keep boundary handling and index arithmetic out of the SIMD loop.
+inline double computeLaplacian(const double* row, const double* yn, const double* yp,
+                              const double* zn, const double* zp,
+                              const size_t x, const size_t xn, const size_t xp,
+                              const double dx2, const double dy2, const double dz2) noexcept {

-void computeChemicalPotential(const std::vector<double>& c, std::vector<double>& mu,

+// The potential tile, its concentration data and its output fit in a private
+// CPU cache. Halos contain real neighboring cells, with clamping only at the
+// physical domain boundary.
+constexpr size_t tileX = 128;
+constexpr size_t tileY = 8;
+constexpr size_t tileZ = 8;
+
+// Apply a stencil to part of a row. Only physical X boundaries need scalar
+// handling; both tile interiors and their halos use contiguous SIMD loads.
+template <typename CellOperation>
+void stencilRow(const double* row, const double* yn, const double* yp,
+                const double* zn, const double* zp, double* __restrict__ result,
+                const size_t xBegin, const size_t xEnd, const size_t nx,
+                const size_t fieldXBegin,
+                const double dx2, const double dy2, const double dz2,
+                CellOperation operation) {
+    const auto apply = [&](const size_t x, const size_t xn, const size_t xp) {
+        const size_t localX = x - fieldXBegin;
+        return operation(x, row[localX],
+                         computeLaplacian(row, yn, yp, zn, zp, localX,
+                                          xn - fieldXBegin, xp - fieldXBegin, dx2, dy2, dz2));
+    };
+
+    const size_t interiorBegin = std::max(xBegin, size_t{1});
+    const size_t interiorEnd = std::min(xEnd, nx - 1);
+    if (xBegin == 0) {
+        result[0] = apply(0, 0, nx > 1 ? 1 : 0);
+    }
+    #pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t x = interiorBegin; x < interiorEnd; ++x) {
+        result[x - xBegin] = apply(x, x - 1, x + 1);
     }
+    if (xEnd == nx && nx > 1) {
+        result[nx - 1 - xBegin] = apply(nx - 1, nx - 2, nx - 1);
+    }
 }
 
-// Cahn-Hilliard update step
-void cahnHilliardUpdate(std::vector<double>& cnew, const std::vector<double>& cold,
-                        const std::vector<double>& mu,
-                        const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
-                        const double D, const double dt, const double dx, const double dy, const double dz) {
-    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
-        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
-            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
-                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
-                cnew[idx] = cold[idx] + dt * D * 
-                           computeLaplacian(mu, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, x, y, z);
-            }
+// Compute a tile's chemical potential, including a one-cell halo, directly from
+// the immutable old concentration. Then update just the tile's owned cells.
+// Potentials are thread-private, so tiles need no intermediate global barrier.
+void advanceTile(const double* __restrict__ cold, double* __restrict__ cnew,
+                 double* __restrict__ mu,
+                 const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
+                 const size_t xBegin, const size_t xEnd,
+                 const size_t yBegin, const size_t yEnd,
+                 const size_t zBegin, const size_t zEnd,
+                 const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,
+                 const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB,
+                 const double D, const double dt) {
+    const size_t hxBegin = xBegin > 0 ? xBegin - 1 : 0;
+    const size_t hxEnd = xEnd < nx ? xEnd + 1 : nx;
+    const size_t hyBegin = yBegin > 0 ? yBegin - 1 : 0;
+    const size_t hyEnd = yEnd < ny ? yEnd + 1 : ny;
+    const size_t hzBegin = zBegin > 0 ? zBegin - 1 : 0;
+    const size_t hzEnd = zEnd < nz ? zEnd + 1 : nz;
+    const size_t muStride = hxEnd - hxBegin;
+    const size_t muPlane = muStride * (hyEnd - hyBegin);
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const double dx2 = dx * dx;
+    const double dy2 = dy * dy;
+    const double dz2 = dz * dz;
+
+    for (size_t z = hzBegin; z < hzEnd; ++z) {
+        for (size_t y = hyBegin; y < hyEnd; ++y) {
+            const double* row = cold + z * plane + y * nx;
+            double* potentialRow = mu + (z - hzBegin) * muPlane + (y - hyBegin) * muStride;
+            stencilRow(row, row - (y > 0 ? nx : 0), row + (y + 1 < ny ? nx : 0),
+                       row - (z > 0 ? plane : 0), row + (z + 1 < nz ? plane : 0),
+                       potentialRow, hxBegin, hxEnd, nx, 0, dx2, dy2, dz2,
+                       [=](size_t, const double cv, const double laplacian) {
+                           return 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB)
+                                  + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv - gamma * laplacian;
+                       });
+        }
+    }
+
+    for (size_t z = zBegin; z < zEnd; ++z) {
+        for (size_t y = yBegin; y < yEnd; ++y) {
+            const double* oldRow = cold + z * plane + y * nx;
+            const double* row = mu + (z - hzBegin) * muPlane + (y - hyBegin) * muStride;
+            double* nextRow = cnew + z * plane + y * nx + xBegin;
+            stencilRow(row, row - (y > 0 ? muStride : 0), row + (y + 1 < ny ? muStride : 0),
+                       row - (z > 0 ? muPlane : 0), row + (z + 1 < nz ? muPlane : 0),
+                       nextRow, xBegin, xEnd, nx, hxBegin, dx2, dy2, dz2,
+                       [=](const size_t x, double, const double laplacian) {
+                           return oldRow[x] + dt * D * laplacian;
+                       });
         }
     }
 }

+inline double computeLaplacian(const double* row, const double* yn, const double* yp,
+                              const double* zn, const double* zp,
+                              const size_t x, const size_t xn, const size_t xp,
