+// The potential tile, its concentration data and its output fit in a private
+// CPU cache. Halos contain real neighboring cells, with clamping only at the
+// physical domain boundary.
+constexpr size_t tileX = 128;
+constexpr size_t tileY = 8;
+constexpr size_t tileZ = 8;
+
+// Apply a stencil to part of a row. Only physical X boundaries need scalar
+// handling; both tile interiors and their halos use contiguous SIMD loads.
+template <typename CellOperation>
+void stencilRow(const double* row, const double* yn, const double* yp,
+                const double* zn, const double* zp, double* __restrict__ result,
+                const size_t xBegin, const size_t xEnd, const size_t nx,
+                const size_t fieldXBegin,
+                const double dx2, const double dy2, const double dz2,
+                CellOperation operation) {
+    const auto apply = [&](const size_t x, const size_t xn, const size_t xp) {
+        const size_t localX = x - fieldXBegin;
+        return operation(x, row[localX],
+                         computeLaplacian(row, yn, yp, zn, zp, localX,
+                                          xn - fieldXBegin, xp - fieldXBegin, dx2, dy2, dz2));
+    };
+
+    const size_t interiorBegin = std::max(xBegin, size_t{1});
+    const size_t interiorEnd = std::min(xEnd, nx - 1);
+    if (xBegin == 0) {
+        result[0] = apply(0, 0, nx > 1 ? 1 : 0);
+    }
+    #pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t x = interiorBegin; x < interiorEnd; ++x) {
+        result[x - xBegin] = apply(x, x - 1, x + 1);
     }
+    if (xEnd == nx && nx > 1) {
+        result[nx - 1 - xBegin] = apply(nx - 1, nx - 2, nx - 1);
+    }
 }

-void cahnHilliardUpdate(std::vector<double>& cnew, const std::vector<double>& cold,

-                        const double D, const double dt, const double dx, const double dy, const double dz) {

+// Compute a tile's chemical potential, including a one-cell halo, directly from
+// the immutable old concentration. Then update just the tile's owned cells.
+// Potentials are thread-private, so tiles need no intermediate global barrier.
+void advanceTile(const double* __restrict__ cold, double* __restrict__ cnew,
+                 double* __restrict__ mu,
+                 const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
+                 const size_t xBegin, const size_t xEnd,
+                 const size_t yBegin, const size_t yEnd,
+                 const size_t zBegin, const size_t zEnd,
+                 const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,
+                 const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB,
+                 const double D, const double dt) {
+    const size_t hxBegin = xBegin > 0 ? xBegin - 1 : 0;
+    const size_t hxEnd = xEnd < nx ? xEnd + 1 : nx;
+    const size_t hyBegin = yBegin > 0 ? yBegin - 1 : 0;
+    const size_t hyEnd = yEnd < ny ? yEnd + 1 : ny;
+    const size_t hzBegin = zBegin > 0 ? zBegin - 1 : 0;
+    const size_t hzEnd = zEnd < nz ? zEnd + 1 : nz;
+    const size_t muStride = hxEnd - hxBegin;
+    const size_t muPlane = muStride * (hyEnd - hyBegin);
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const double dx2 = dx * dx;
+    const double dy2 = dy * dy;
+    const double dz2 = dz * dz;
+
+    for (size_t z = hzBegin; z < hzEnd; ++z) {
+        for (size_t y = hyBegin; y < hyEnd; ++y) {
+            const double* row = cold + z * plane + y * nx;
+            double* potentialRow = mu + (z - hzBegin) * muPlane + (y - hyBegin) * muStride;
+            stencilRow(row, row - (y > 0 ? nx : 0), row + (y + 1 < ny ? nx : 0),
+                       row - (z > 0 ? plane : 0), row + (z + 1 < nz ? plane : 0),
+                       potentialRow, hxBegin, hxEnd, nx, 0, dx2, dy2, dz2,
+                       [=](size_t, const double cv, const double laplacian) {
+                           return 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB)
+                                  + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv - gamma * laplacian;
+                       });
+        }
+    }
+
+    for (size_t z = zBegin; z < zEnd; ++z) {
+        for (size_t y = yBegin; y < yEnd; ++y) {
+            const double* oldRow = cold + z * plane + y * nx;
+            const double* row = mu + (z - hzBegin) * muPlane + (y - hyBegin) * muStride;
+            double* nextRow = cnew + z * plane + y * nx + xBegin;
+            stencilRow(row, row - (y > 0 ? muStride : 0), row + (y + 1 < ny ? muStride : 0),
+                       row - (z > 0 ? muPlane : 0), row + (z + 1 < nz ? muPlane : 0),
+                       nextRow, xBegin, xEnd, nx, hxBegin, dx2, dy2, dz2,
+                       [=](const size_t x, double, const double laplacian) {
+                           return oldRow[x] + dt * D * laplacian;
+                       });
         }
     }
 }

+// The potential tile, its concentration data and its output fit in a private
+// CPU cache. Halos contain real neighboring cells, with clamping only at the
+// physical domain boundary.
+constexpr size_t tileX = 128;
+constexpr size_t tileY = 8;
+constexpr size_t tileZ = 8;
+
+// Apply a stencil to part of a row. Only physical X boundaries need scalar
+// handling; both tile interiors and their halos use contiguous SIMD loads.
+template <typename CellOperation>
+void stencilRow(const double* row, const double* yn, const double* yp,
+                const double* zn, const double* zp, double* __restrict__ result,
+                const size_t xBegin, const size_t xEnd, const size_t nx,
+                const size_t fieldXBegin,
+                const double dx2, const double dy2, const double dz2,
+                CellOperation operation) {
+    const auto apply = [&](const size_t x, const size_t xn, const size_t xp) {
+        const size_t localX = x - fieldXBegin;
+        return operation(x, row[localX],
+                         computeLaplacian(row, yn, yp, zn, zp, localX,
+                                          xn - fieldXBegin, xp - fieldXBegin, dx2, dy2, dz2));
+    };
+
+    const size_t interiorBegin = std::max(xBegin, size_t{1});
+    const size_t interiorEnd = std::min(xEnd, nx - 1);
+    if (xBegin == 0) {
+        result[0] = apply(0, 0, nx > 1 ? 1 : 0);
+    }
+    #pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t x = interiorBegin; x < interiorEnd; ++x) {
+        result[x - xBegin] = apply(x, x - 1, x + 1);
     }
+    if (xEnd == nx && nx > 1) {
+        result[nx - 1 - xBegin] = apply(nx - 1, nx - 2, nx - 1);
+    }
 }
 
-// Cahn-Hilliard update step
-void cahnHilliardUpdate(std::vector<double>& cnew, const std::vector<double>& cold,
-                        const std::vector<double>& mu,
-                        const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
-                        const double D, const double dt, const double dx, const double dy, const double dz) {
-    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
-        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
-            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
-                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
-                cnew[idx] = cold[idx] + dt * D * 
-                           computeLaplacian(mu, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, x, y, z);
-            }
+// Compute a tile's chemical potential, including a one-cell halo, directly from
+// the immutable old concentration. Then update just the tile's owned cells.
+// Potentials are thread-private, so tiles need no intermediate global barrier.
+void advanceTile(const double* __restrict__ cold, double* __restrict__ cnew,
+                 double* __restrict__ mu,
+                 const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
+                 const size_t xBegin, const size_t xEnd,
+                 const size_t yBegin, const size_t yEnd,
+                 const size_t zBegin, const size_t zEnd,
+                 const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,
+                 const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB,
+                 const double D, const double dt) {
+    const size_t hxBegin = xBegin > 0 ? xBegin - 1 : 0;
+    const size_t hxEnd = xEnd < nx ? xEnd + 1 : nx;
+    const size_t hyBegin = yBegin > 0 ? yBegin - 1 : 0;
+    const size_t hyEnd = yEnd < ny ? yEnd + 1 : ny;
+    const size_t hzBegin = zBegin > 0 ? zBegin - 1 : 0;
+    const size_t hzEnd = zEnd < nz ? zEnd + 1 : nz;
+    const size_t muStride = hxEnd - hxBegin;
+    const size_t muPlane = muStride * (hyEnd - hyBegin);
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const double dx2 = dx * dx;
+    const double dy2 = dy * dy;
+    const double dz2 = dz * dz;
+
+    for (size_t z = hzBegin; z < hzEnd; ++z) {
+        for (size_t y = hyBegin; y < hyEnd; ++y) {
+            const double* row = cold + z * plane + y * nx;
+            double* potentialRow = mu + (z - hzBegin) * muPlane + (y - hyBegin) * muStride;
+            stencilRow(row, row - (y > 0 ? nx : 0), row + (y + 1 < ny ? nx : 0),
+                       row - (z > 0 ? plane : 0), row + (z + 1 < nz ? plane : 0),
+                       potentialRow, hxBegin, hxEnd, nx, 0, dx2, dy2, dz2,
+                       [=](size_t, const double cv, const double laplacian) {
+                           return 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB)
+                                  + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv - gamma * laplacian;
+                       });
+        }
+    }
+
+    for (size_t z = zBegin; z < zEnd; ++z) {
+        for (size_t y = yBegin; y < yEnd; ++y) {
+            const double* oldRow = cold + z * plane + y * nx;
+            const double* row = mu + (z - hzBegin) * muPlane + (y - hyBegin) * muStride;
+            double* nextRow = cnew + z * plane + y * nx + xBegin;
+            stencilRow(row, row - (y > 0 ? muStride : 0), row + (y + 1 < ny ? muStride : 0),
+                       row - (z > 0 ? muPlane : 0), row + (z + 1 < nz ? muPlane : 0),
+                       nextRow, xBegin, xEnd, nx, hxBegin, dx2, dy2, dz2,
+                       [=](const size_t x, double, const double laplacian) {
+                           return oldRow[x] + dt * D * laplacian;
+                       });
         }
     }
 }

+                 const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB,
+                 const double D, const double dt) {
+    const size_t hxBegin = xBegin > 0 ? xBegin - 1 : 0;
+    const size_t hxEnd = xEnd < nx ? xEnd + 1 : nx;
+    const size_t hyBegin = yBegin > 0 ? yBegin - 1 : 0;
+    const size_t hyEnd = yEnd < ny ? yEnd + 1 : ny;
+    const size_t hzBegin = zBegin > 0 ? zBegin - 1 : 0;
+    const size_t hzEnd = zEnd < nz ? zEnd + 1 : nz;
+    const size_t muStride = hxEnd - hxBegin;
+    const size_t muPlane = muStride * (hyEnd - hyBegin);
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const double dx2 = dx * dx;
+    const double dy2 = dy * dy;
+    const double dz2 = dz * dz;
+
+    for (size_t z = hzBegin; z < hzEnd; ++z) {
+        for (size_t y = hyBegin; y < hyEnd; ++y) {
+            const double* row = cold + z * plane + y * nx;
+            double* potentialRow = mu + (z - hzBegin) * muPlane + (y - hyBegin) * muStride;
+            stencilRow(row, row - (y > 0 ? nx : 0), row + (y + 1 < ny ? nx : 0),
+                       row - (z > 0 ? plane : 0), row + (z + 1 < nz ? plane : 0),
+                       potentialRow, hxBegin, hxEnd, nx, 0, dx2, dy2, dz2,
+                       [=](size_t, const double cv, const double laplacian) {
+                           return 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB)
+                                  + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv - gamma * laplacian;
+                       });
+        }
+    }
+
+    for (size_t z = zBegin; z < zEnd; ++z) {
+        for (size_t y = yBegin; y < yEnd; ++y) {
+            const double* oldRow = cold + z * plane + y * nx;
+            const double* row = mu + (z - hzBegin) * muPlane + (y - hyBegin) * muStride;
+            double* nextRow = cnew + z * plane + y * nx + xBegin;
+            stencilRow(row, row - (y > 0 ? muStride : 0), row + (y + 1 < ny ? muStride : 0),
+                       row - (z > 0 ? muPlane : 0), row + (z + 1 < nz ? muPlane : 0),
+                       nextRow, xBegin, xEnd, nx, hxBegin, dx2, dy2, dz2,
+                       [=](const size_t x, double, const double laplacian) {
+                           return oldRow[x] + dt * D * laplacian;
+                       });
         }
     }
 }

codex
Tile size affects cache use and the amount of repeated halo work. The smaller tiles scale more consistently so far. The tiled solver also uses two full-grid concentration buffers and a small private potential buffer per thread, instead of three full-grid buffers.

+inline double computeLaplacian(const double* row, const double* yn, const double* yp,

+// The potential tile, its concentration data and its output fit in a private
+// CPU cache. Halos contain real neighboring cells, with clamping only at the
+// physical domain boundary.
+constexpr size_t tileX = 128;
+constexpr size_t tileY = 8;
+constexpr size_t tileZ = 8;
+
+// Apply a stencil to part of a row. Only physical X boundaries need scalar
+// handling; both tile interiors and their halos use contiguous SIMD loads.
+template <typename CellOperation>
+void stencilRow(const double* row, const double* yn, const double* yp,
+                const double* zn, const double* zp, double* __restrict__ result,
+                const size_t xBegin, const size_t xEnd, const size_t nx,
+                const size_t fieldXBegin,
+                const double dx2, const double dy2, const double dz2,
+                CellOperation operation) {
+    const auto apply = [&](const size_t x, const size_t xn, const size_t xp) {
+        const size_t localX = x - fieldXBegin;
+        return operation(x, row[localX],
+                         computeLaplacian(row, yn, yp, zn, zp, localX,
+                                          xn - fieldXBegin, xp - fieldXBegin, dx2, dy2, dz2));
+    };
+
+    const size_t interiorBegin = std::max(xBegin, size_t{1});
+    const size_t interiorEnd = std::min(xEnd, nx - 1);
+    if (xBegin == 0) {
+        result[0] = apply(0, 0, nx > 1 ? 1 : 0);
+    }
+    #pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t x = interiorBegin; x < interiorEnd; ++x) {
+        result[x - xBegin] = apply(x, x - 1, x + 1);
     }
+    if (xEnd == nx && nx > 1) {
+        result[nx - 1 - xBegin] = apply(nx - 1, nx - 2, nx - 1);
+    }
 }

+// Compute a tile's chemical potential, including a one-cell halo, directly from
+// the immutable old concentration. Then update just the tile's owned cells.
+// Potentials are thread-private, so tiles need no intermediate global barrier.
+void advanceTile(const double* __restrict__ cold, double* __restrict__ cnew,
+                 double* __restrict__ mu,
+                 const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
+                 const size_t xBegin, const size_t xEnd,
+                 const size_t yBegin, const size_t yEnd,
+                 const size_t zBegin, const size_t zEnd,
+                 const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,
+                 const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB,
+                 const double D, const double dt) {
+    const size_t hxBegin = xBegin > 0 ? xBegin - 1 : 0;
+    const size_t hxEnd = xEnd < nx ? xEnd + 1 : nx;
+    const size_t hyBegin = yBegin > 0 ? yBegin - 1 : 0;
+    const size_t hyEnd = yEnd < ny ? yEnd + 1 : ny;
+    const size_t hzBegin = zBegin > 0 ? zBegin - 1 : 0;
+    const size_t hzEnd = zEnd < nz ? zEnd + 1 : nz;
+    const size_t muStride = hxEnd - hxBegin;
+    const size_t muPlane = muStride * (hyEnd - hyBegin);
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const double dx2 = dx * dx;
+    const double dy2 = dy * dy;
+    const double dz2 = dz * dz;
+
+    for (size_t z = hzBegin; z < hzEnd; ++z) {
+        for (size_t y = hyBegin; y < hyEnd; ++y) {
+            const double* row = cold + z * plane + y * nx;
+            double* potentialRow = mu + (z - hzBegin) * muPlane + (y - hyBegin) * muStride;
+            stencilRow(row, row - (y > 0 ? nx : 0), row + (y + 1 < ny ? nx : 0),
+                       row - (z > 0 ? plane : 0), row + (z + 1 < nz ? plane : 0),
+                       potentialRow, hxBegin, hxEnd, nx, 0, dx2, dy2, dz2,
+                       [=](size_t, const double cv, const double laplacian) {
+                           return 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB)
+                                  + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv - gamma * laplacian;
+                       });
+        }
+    }
+
+    for (size_t z = zBegin; z < zEnd; ++z) {
+        for (size_t y = yBegin; y < yEnd; ++y) {
+            const double* oldRow = cold + z * plane + y * nx;
+            const double* row = mu + (z - hzBegin) * muPlane + (y - hyBegin) * muStride;
+            double* nextRow = cnew + z * plane + y * nx + xBegin;
+            stencilRow(row, row - (y > 0 ? muStride : 0), row + (y + 1 < ny ? muStride : 0),
+                       row - (z > 0 ? muPlane : 0), row + (z + 1 < nz ? muPlane : 0),
+                       nextRow, xBegin, xEnd, nx, hxBegin, dx2, dy2, dz2,
+                       [=](const size_t x, double, const double laplacian) {
+                           return oldRow[x] + dt * D * laplacian;
+                       });
         }
     }
 }

+// Row pointers keep boundary handling and index arithmetic out of the SIMD loop.
+inline double computeLaplacian(const double* row, const double* yn, const double* yp,
+                              const double* zn, const double* zp,
+                              const size_t x, const size_t xn, const size_t xp,
+                              const double dx2, const double dy2, const double dz2) noexcept {
+    const double cv = row[x];
+    const double cxx = (row[xp] + row[xn] - 2.0 * cv) / dx2;

-void computeChemicalPotential(const std::vector<double>& c, std::vector<double>& mu,

-                              const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,

+// The potential tile, its concentration data and its output fit in a private
+// CPU cache. Halos contain real neighboring cells, with clamping only at the
+// physical domain boundary.
+constexpr size_t tileX = 128;
+constexpr size_t tileY = 8;
+constexpr size_t tileZ = 8;
+
+// Apply a stencil to part of a row. Only physical X boundaries need scalar
+// handling; both tile interiors and their halos use contiguous SIMD loads.
+template <typename CellOperation>
+void stencilRow(const double* row, const double* yn, const double* yp,
+                const double* zn, const double* zp, double* __restrict__ result,
+                const size_t xBegin, const size_t xEnd, const size_t nx,
+                const size_t fieldXBegin,
+                const double dx2, const double dy2, const double dz2,
+                CellOperation operation) {
+    const auto apply = [&](const size_t x, const size_t xn, const size_t xp) {
+        const size_t localX = x - fieldXBegin;
+        return operation(x, row[localX],
+                         computeLaplacian(row, yn, yp, zn, zp, localX,
+                                          xn - fieldXBegin, xp - fieldXBegin, dx2, dy2, dz2));
+    };
+
+    const size_t interiorBegin = std::max(xBegin, size_t{1});
+    const size_t interiorEnd = std::min(xEnd, nx - 1);
+    if (xBegin == 0) {
+        result[0] = apply(0, 0, nx > 1 ? 1 : 0);
+    }
+    #pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t x = interiorBegin; x < interiorEnd; ++x) {
+        result[x - xBegin] = apply(x, x - 1, x + 1);
     }
+    if (xEnd == nx && nx > 1) {
+        result[nx - 1 - xBegin] = apply(nx - 1, nx - 2, nx - 1);
+    }
 }
 
-// Cahn-Hilliard update step
-void cahnHilliardUpdate(std::vector<double>& cnew, const std::vector<double>& cold,
-                        const std::vector<double>& mu,
-                        const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
-                        const double D, const double dt, const double dx, const double dy, const double dz) {
-    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
-        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
-            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
-                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
-                cnew[idx] = cold[idx] + dt * D * 
-                           computeLaplacian(mu, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, x, y, z);
-            }
+// Compute a tile's chemical potential, including a one-cell halo, directly from
+// the immutable old concentration. Then update just the tile's owned cells.
+// Potentials are thread-private, so tiles need no intermediate global barrier.
+void advanceTile(const double* __restrict__ cold, double* __restrict__ cnew,
+                 double* __restrict__ mu,
+                 const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
+                 const size_t xBegin, const size_t xEnd,
+                 const size_t yBegin, const size_t yEnd,
+                 const size_t zBegin, const size_t zEnd,
+                 const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,
+                 const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB,
+                 const double D, const double dt) {
+    const size_t hxBegin = xBegin > 0 ? xBegin - 1 : 0;
+    const size_t hxEnd = xEnd < nx ? xEnd + 1 : nx;
+    const size_t hyBegin = yBegin > 0 ? yBegin - 1 : 0;
+    const size_t hyEnd = yEnd < ny ? yEnd + 1 : ny;
+    const size_t hzBegin = zBegin > 0 ? zBegin - 1 : 0;
+    const size_t hzEnd = zEnd < nz ? zEnd + 1 : nz;
+    const size_t muStride = hxEnd - hxBegin;
+    const size_t muPlane = muStride * (hyEnd - hyBegin);
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const double dx2 = dx * dx;
+    const double dy2 = dy * dy;
+    const double dz2 = dz * dz;
+
+    for (size_t z = hzBegin; z < hzEnd; ++z) {
+        for (size_t y = hyBegin; y < hyEnd; ++y) {
+            const double* row = cold + z * plane + y * nx;
+            double* potentialRow = mu + (z - hzBegin) * muPlane + (y - hyBegin) * muStride;
+            stencilRow(row, row - (y > 0 ? nx : 0), row + (y + 1 < ny ? nx : 0),
+                       row - (z > 0 ? plane : 0), row + (z + 1 < nz ? plane : 0),
+                       potentialRow, hxBegin, hxEnd, nx, 0, dx2, dy2, dz2,
+                       [=](size_t, const double cv, const double laplacian) {
+                           return 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB)
+                                  + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv - gamma * laplacian;
+                       });
+        }
+    }
+
+    for (size_t z = zBegin; z < zEnd; ++z) {
+        for (size_t y = yBegin; y < yEnd; ++y) {
+            const double* oldRow = cold + z * plane + y * nx;
+            const double* row = mu + (z - hzBegin) * muPlane + (y - hyBegin) * muStride;
+            double* nextRow = cnew + z * plane + y * nx + xBegin;
+            stencilRow(row, row - (y > 0 ? muStride : 0), row + (y + 1 < ny ? muStride : 0),
+                       row - (z > 0 ? muPlane : 0), row + (z + 1 < nz ? muPlane : 0),
+                       nextRow, xBegin, xEnd, nx, hxBegin, dx2, dy2, dz2,
+                       [=](const size_t x, double, const double laplacian) {
+                           return oldRow[x] + dt * D * laplacian;
+                       });
         }
     }
 }

-    const size_t yp = (y < ny - 1) ? y + 1 : y;

+// Row pointers keep boundary handling and index arithmetic out of the SIMD loop.
+inline double computeLaplacian(const double* row, const double* yn, const double* yp,
+                              const double* zn, const double* zp,
+                              const size_t x, const size_t xn, const size_t xp,

+// The potential tile, its concentration data and its output fit in a private
+// CPU cache. Halos contain real neighboring cells, with clamping only at the
+// physical domain boundary.
+struct TileShape {
+    size_t x, y, z;
+};
+
+inline size_t tileCount(const size_t extent, const size_t width) noexcept {
+    return extent / width + (extent % width != 0);
+}
+
+TileShape chooseTileShape(const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {
+    TileShape shape{std::max(size_t{1}, std::min(nx, size_t{512})),
+                    std::max(size_t{1}, std::min(ny, size_t{8})),
+                    std::max(size_t{1}, std::min(nz, size_t{8}))};
+    // Keep enough independent tiles for the requested team, including thin
+    // grids. Prefer wide X rows for efficient SIMD and contiguous memory access.
+    const size_t threads = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_max_threads());
+    while (tileCount(nx, shape.x) * tileCount(ny, shape.y) * tileCount(nz, shape.z) < threads) {
+        if (shape.y >= shape.z && shape.y > 1) {
+            shape.y = (shape.y + 1) / 2;
+        } else if (shape.z > 1) {
+            shape.z = (shape.z + 1) / 2;
+        } else if (shape.x > 1) {
+            shape.x = (shape.x + 1) / 2;
+        } else {
+            break;
         }
     }
+    return shape;
 }
 
-// Cahn-Hilliard update step
-void cahnHilliardUpdate(std::vector<double>& cnew, const std::vector<double>& cold,
-                        const std::vector<double>& mu,
-                        const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
-                        const double D, const double dt, const double dx, const double dy, const double dz) {
-    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
-        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
-            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {

-                cnew[idx] = cold[idx] + dt * D * 
-                           computeLaplacian(mu, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, x, y, z);
-            }
+// Apply a stencil to part of a row. Only physical X boundaries need scalar
+// handling; both tile interiors and their halos use contiguous SIMD loads.
+template <typename CellOperation>
+void stencilRow(const double* row, const double* yn, const double* yp,
+                const double* zn, const double* zp, double* __restrict__ result,
+                const size_t xBegin, const size_t xEnd, const size_t nx,
+                const size_t fieldXBegin,
+                const double dx2, const double dy2, const double dz2,
+                CellOperation operation) {
+    const auto apply = [&](const size_t x, const size_t xn, const size_t xp) {
+        const size_t localX = x - fieldXBegin;
+        return operation(x, row[localX],
+                         computeLaplacian(row, yn, yp, zn, zp, localX,
+                                          xn - fieldXBegin, xp - fieldXBegin, dx2, dy2, dz2));
+    };
+
+    const size_t interiorBegin = std::max(xBegin, size_t{1});
+    const size_t interiorEnd = std::min(xEnd, nx - 1);
+    if (xBegin == 0) {
+        result[0] = apply(0, 0, nx > 1 ? 1 : 0);
+    }
+    #pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t x = interiorBegin; x < interiorEnd; ++x) {
+        result[x - xBegin] = apply(x, x - 1, x + 1);
+    }
+    if (xEnd == nx && nx > 1) {
+        result[nx - 1 - xBegin] = apply(nx - 1, nx - 2, nx - 1);
+    }
+}
+
+// Compute a tile's chemical potential, including a one-cell halo, directly from
+// the immutable old concentration. Then update just the tile's owned cells.
+// Potentials are thread-private, so tiles need no intermediate global barrier.
+void advanceTile(const double* __restrict__ cold, double* __restrict__ cnew,
+                 double* __restrict__ mu,
+                 const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
+                 const size_t xBegin, const size_t xEnd,
+                 const size_t yBegin, const size_t yEnd,
+                 const size_t zBegin, const size_t zEnd,
+                 const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,
+                 const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB,
+                 const double D, const double dt) {
+    const size_t hxBegin = xBegin > 0 ? xBegin - 1 : 0;
+    const size_t hxEnd = xEnd < nx ? xEnd + 1 : nx;
+    const size_t hyBegin = yBegin > 0 ? yBegin - 1 : 0;
+    const size_t hyEnd = yEnd < ny ? yEnd + 1 : ny;
+    const size_t hzBegin = zBegin > 0 ? zBegin - 1 : 0;
+    const size_t hzEnd = zEnd < nz ? zEnd + 1 : nz;
+    const size_t muStride = hxEnd - hxBegin;
+    const size_t muPlane = muStride * (hyEnd - hyBegin);
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const double dx2 = dx * dx;
+    const double dy2 = dy * dy;
+    const double dz2 = dz * dz;
+
+    for (size_t z = hzBegin; z < hzEnd; ++z) {
+        for (size_t y = hyBegin; y < hyEnd; ++y) {
+            const double* row = cold + z * plane + y * nx;
+            double* potentialRow = mu + (z - hzBegin) * muPlane + (y - hyBegin) * muStride;
+            stencilRow(row, row - (y > 0 ? nx : 0), row + (y + 1 < ny ? nx : 0),
+                       row - (z > 0 ? plane : 0), row + (z + 1 < nz ? plane : 0),
+                       potentialRow, hxBegin, hxEnd, nx, 0, dx2, dy2, dz2,
+                       [=](size_t, const double cv, const double laplacian) {
+                           return 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB)
+                                  + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv - gamma * laplacian;
+                       });
         }
     }
+
+    for (size_t z = zBegin; z < zEnd; ++z) {
+        for (size_t y = yBegin; y < yEnd; ++y) {
+            const double* oldRow = cold + z * plane + y * nx;
+            const double* row = mu + (z - hzBegin) * muPlane + (y - hyBegin) * muStride;
+            double* nextRow = cnew + z * plane + y * nx + xBegin;
+            stencilRow(row, row - (y > 0 ? muStride : 0), row + (y + 1 < ny ? muStride : 0),
+                       row - (z > 0 ? muPlane : 0), row + (z + 1 < nz ? muPlane : 0),
+                       nextRow, xBegin, xEnd, nx, hxBegin, dx2, dy2, dz2,
+                       [=](const size_t x, double, const double laplacian) {
+                           return oldRow[x] + dt * D * laplacian;
+                       });
+        }
+    }
 }

-            }
+// The potential tile, its concentration data and its output fit in a private
+// CPU cache. Halos contain real neighboring cells, with clamping only at the
+// physical domain boundary.
+struct TileShape {
+    size_t x, y, z;
+};
+
+inline size_t tileCount(const size_t extent, const size_t width) noexcept {
+    return extent / width + (extent % width != 0);
+}
+
+TileShape chooseTileShape(const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {
+    TileShape shape{std::max(size_t{1}, std::min(nx, size_t{512})),
+                    std::max(size_t{1}, std::min(ny, size_t{8})),
+                    std::max(size_t{1}, std::min(nz, size_t{8}))};
+    // Keep enough independent tiles for the requested team, including thin
+    // grids. Prefer wide X rows for efficient SIMD and contiguous memory access.
+    const size_t threads = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_max_threads());
+    while (tileCount(nx, shape.x) * tileCount(ny, shape.y) * tileCount(nz, shape.z) < threads) {
+        if (shape.y >= shape.z && shape.y > 1) {
+            shape.y = (shape.y + 1) / 2;
+        } else if (shape.z > 1) {
+            shape.z = (shape.z + 1) / 2;
+        } else if (shape.x > 1) {
+            shape.x = (shape.x + 1) / 2;
+        } else {
+            break;
         }
     }
+    return shape;
 }
 
-// Cahn-Hilliard update step
-void cahnHilliardUpdate(std::vector<double>& cnew, const std::vector<double>& cold,
-                        const std::vector<double>& mu,
-                        const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
-                        const double D, const double dt, const double dx, const double dy, const double dz) {
-    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
-        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
-            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
-                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
-                cnew[idx] = cold[idx] + dt * D * 
-                           computeLaplacian(mu, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, x, y, z);
-            }
+// Apply a stencil to part of a row. Only physical X boundaries need scalar
+// handling; both tile interiors and their halos use contiguous SIMD loads.
+template <typename CellOperation>
+void stencilRow(const double* row, const double* yn, const double* yp,
+                const double* zn, const double* zp, double* __restrict__ result,
+                const size_t xBegin, const size_t xEnd, const size_t nx,
+                const size_t fieldXBegin,
+                const double dx2, const double dy2, const double dz2,
+                CellOperation operation) {
+    const auto apply = [&](const size_t x, const size_t xn, const size_t xp) {
+        const size_t localX = x - fieldXBegin;
+        return operation(x, row[localX],
+                         computeLaplacian(row, yn, yp, zn, zp, localX,
+                                          xn - fieldXBegin, xp - fieldXBegin, dx2, dy2, dz2));
+    };
+
+    const size_t interiorBegin = std::max(xBegin, size_t{1});
+    const size_t interiorEnd = std::min(xEnd, nx - 1);
+    if (xBegin == 0) {
+        result[0] = apply(0, 0, nx > 1 ? 1 : 0);
+    }
+    #pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t x = interiorBegin; x < interiorEnd; ++x) {
+        result[x - xBegin] = apply(x, x - 1, x + 1);
+    }
+    if (xEnd == nx && nx > 1) {
+        result[nx - 1 - xBegin] = apply(nx - 1, nx - 2, nx - 1);
+    }
+}
+
+// Compute a tile's chemical potential, including a one-cell halo, directly from
+// the immutable old concentration. Then update just the tile's owned cells.
+// Potentials are thread-private, so tiles need no intermediate global barrier.
+void advanceTile(const double* __restrict__ cold, double* __restrict__ cnew,
+                 double* __restrict__ mu,
+                 const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
+                 const size_t xBegin, const size_t xEnd,
+                 const size_t yBegin, const size_t yEnd,
+                 const size_t zBegin, const size_t zEnd,
+                 const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,
+                 const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB,
+                 const double D, const double dt) {
+    const size_t hxBegin = xBegin > 0 ? xBegin - 1 : 0;
+    const size_t hxEnd = xEnd < nx ? xEnd + 1 : nx;
+    const size_t hyBegin = yBegin > 0 ? yBegin - 1 : 0;
+    const size_t hyEnd = yEnd < ny ? yEnd + 1 : ny;
+    const size_t hzBegin = zBegin > 0 ? zBegin - 1 : 0;
+    const size_t hzEnd = zEnd < nz ? zEnd + 1 : nz;
+    const size_t muStride = hxEnd - hxBegin;
+    const size_t muPlane = muStride * (hyEnd - hyBegin);
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const double dx2 = dx * dx;
+    const double dy2 = dy * dy;
+    const double dz2 = dz * dz;
+
+    for (size_t z = hzBegin; z < hzEnd; ++z) {
+        for (size_t y = hyBegin; y < hyEnd; ++y) {
+            const double* row = cold + z * plane + y * nx;
+            double* potentialRow = mu + (z - hzBegin) * muPlane + (y - hyBegin) * muStride;
+            stencilRow(row, row - (y > 0 ? nx : 0), row + (y + 1 < ny ? nx : 0),
+                       row - (z > 0 ? plane : 0), row + (z + 1 < nz ? plane : 0),
+                       potentialRow, hxBegin, hxEnd, nx, 0, dx2, dy2, dz2,
+                       [=](size_t, const double cv, const double laplacian) {
+                           return 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB)
+                                  + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv - gamma * laplacian;
+                       });
         }
     }
+
+    for (size_t z = zBegin; z < zEnd; ++z) {
+        for (size_t y = yBegin; y < yEnd; ++y) {
+            const double* oldRow = cold + z * plane + y * nx;
+            const double* row = mu + (z - hzBegin) * muPlane + (y - hyBegin) * muStride;
+            double* nextRow = cnew + z * plane + y * nx + xBegin;
+            stencilRow(row, row - (y > 0 ? muStride : 0), row + (y + 1 < ny ? muStride : 0),
+                       row - (z > 0 ? muPlane : 0), row + (z + 1 < nz ? muPlane : 0),
+                       nextRow, xBegin, xEnd, nx, hxBegin, dx2, dy2, dz2,
+                       [=](const size_t x, double, const double laplacian) {
+                           return oldRow[x] + dt * D * laplacian;
+                       });
+        }
+    }
 }

+// The potential tile, its concentration data and its output fit in a private
+// CPU cache. Halos contain real neighboring cells, with clamping only at the

+struct TileShape {

+inline size_t tileCount(const size_t extent, const size_t width) noexcept {

+TileShape chooseTileShape(const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {
+    TileShape shape{std::max(size_t{1}, std::min(nx, size_t{512})),
+                    std::max(size_t{1}, std::min(ny, size_t{8})),
+                    std::max(size_t{1}, std::min(nz, size_t{8}))};

-                           computeLaplacian(mu, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, x, y, z);
-            }
+// Apply a stencil to part of a row. Only physical X boundaries need scalar
+// handling; both tile interiors and their halos use contiguous SIMD loads.
+template <typename CellOperation>
+void stencilRow(const double* row, const double* yn, const double* yp,
+                const double* zn, const double* zp, double* __restrict__ result,
+                const size_t xBegin, const size_t xEnd, const size_t nx,
+                const size_t fieldXBegin,
+                const double dx2, const double dy2, const double dz2,
+                CellOperation operation) {
+    const auto apply = [&](const size_t x, const size_t xn, const size_t xp) {
+        const size_t localX = x - fieldXBegin;
+        return operation(x, row[localX],
+                         computeLaplacian(row, yn, yp, zn, zp, localX,
+                                          xn - fieldXBegin, xp - fieldXBegin, dx2, dy2, dz2));
+    };
+
+    const size_t interiorBegin = std::max(xBegin, size_t{1});
+    const size_t interiorEnd = std::min(xEnd, nx - 1);
+    if (xBegin == 0) {
+        result[0] = apply(0, 0, nx > 1 ? 1 : 0);
+    }
+    #pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t x = interiorBegin; x < interiorEnd; ++x) {
+        result[x - xBegin] = apply(x, x - 1, x + 1);
+    }
+    if (xEnd == nx && nx > 1) {
+        result[nx - 1 - xBegin] = apply(nx - 1, nx - 2, nx - 1);
+    }
+}
+
+// Compute a tile's chemical potential, including a one-cell halo, directly from
+// the immutable old concentration. Then update just the tile's owned cells.
+// Potentials are thread-private, so tiles need no intermediate global barrier.
+void advanceTile(const double* __restrict__ cold, double* __restrict__ cnew,
+                 double* __restrict__ mu,
+                 const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
+                 const size_t xBegin, const size_t xEnd,
+                 const size_t yBegin, const size_t yEnd,
+                 const size_t zBegin, const size_t zEnd,
+                 const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,
+                 const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB,
+                 const double D, const double dt) {
+    const size_t hxBegin = xBegin > 0 ? xBegin - 1 : 0;
+    const size_t hxEnd = xEnd < nx ? xEnd + 1 : nx;
+    const size_t hyBegin = yBegin > 0 ? yBegin - 1 : 0;
+    const size_t hyEnd = yEnd < ny ? yEnd + 1 : ny;
+    const size_t hzBegin = zBegin > 0 ? zBegin - 1 : 0;
+    const size_t hzEnd = zEnd < nz ? zEnd + 1 : nz;
+    const size_t muStride = hxEnd - hxBegin;
+    const size_t muPlane = muStride * (hyEnd - hyBegin);
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const double dx2 = dx * dx;
+    const double dy2 = dy * dy;
+    const double dz2 = dz * dz;
+
+    for (size_t z = hzBegin; z < hzEnd; ++z) {
+        for (size_t y = hyBegin; y < hyEnd; ++y) {
+            const double* row = cold + z * plane + y * nx;
+            double* potentialRow = mu + (z - hzBegin) * muPlane + (y - hyBegin) * muStride;
+            stencilRow(row, row - (y > 0 ? nx : 0), row + (y + 1 < ny ? nx : 0),
+                       row - (z > 0 ? plane : 0), row + (z + 1 < nz ? plane : 0),
+                       potentialRow, hxBegin, hxEnd, nx, 0, dx2, dy2, dz2,
+                       [=](size_t, const double cv, const double laplacian) {
+                           return 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB)
+                                  + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv - gamma * laplacian;
+                       });
         }
     }
+
+    for (size_t z = zBegin; z < zEnd; ++z) {
+        for (size_t y = yBegin; y < yEnd; ++y) {
+            const double* oldRow = cold + z * plane + y * nx;
+            const double* row = mu + (z - hzBegin) * muPlane + (y - hyBegin) * muStride;
+            double* nextRow = cnew + z * plane + y * nx + xBegin;
+            stencilRow(row, row - (y > 0 ? muStride : 0), row + (y + 1 < ny ? muStride : 0),
+                       row - (z > 0 ? muPlane : 0), row + (z + 1 < nz ? muPlane : 0),
+                       nextRow, xBegin, xEnd, nx, hxBegin, dx2, dy2, dz2,
+                       [=](const size_t x, double, const double laplacian) {
+                           return oldRow[x] + dt * D * laplacian;
+                       });
+        }
+    }
 }

+// The potential tile, its concentration data and its output fit in a private
+// CPU cache. Halos contain real neighboring cells, with clamping only at the
+// physical domain boundary.
+struct TileShape {
+    size_t x, y, z;
+};
+
+inline size_t tileCount(const size_t extent, const size_t width) noexcept {
+    return extent / width + (extent % width != 0);
+}
+
+TileShape chooseTileShape(const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {
+    TileShape shape{std::max(size_t{1}, std::min(nx, size_t{512})),
+                    std::max(size_t{1}, std::min(ny, size_t{8})),
+                    std::max(size_t{1}, std::min(nz, size_t{8}))};
+    // Keep enough independent tiles for the requested team, including thin
+    // grids. Prefer wide X rows for efficient SIMD and contiguous memory access.
+    const size_t threads = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_max_threads());
+    while (tileCount(nx, shape.x) * tileCount(ny, shape.y) * tileCount(nz, shape.z) < threads) {
+        if (shape.y >= shape.z && shape.y > 1) {
+            shape.y = (shape.y + 1) / 2;
+        } else if (shape.z > 1) {
+            shape.z = (shape.z + 1) / 2;
+        } else if (shape.x > 1) {
+            shape.x = (shape.x + 1) / 2;
+        } else {
+            break;
         }
     }
+    return shape;
 }

+// Apply a stencil to part of a row. Only physical X boundaries need scalar
+// handling; both tile interiors and their halos use contiguous SIMD loads.
+template <typename CellOperation>
+void stencilRow(const double* row, const double* yn, const double* yp,
+                const double* zn, const double* zp, double* __restrict__ result,
+                const size_t xBegin, const size_t xEnd, const size_t nx,
+                const size_t fieldXBegin,

+        return operation(x, row[localX],
+                         computeLaplacian(row, yn, yp, zn, zp, localX,
+                                          xn - fieldXBegin, xp - fieldXBegin, dx2, dy2, dz2));
+    };
+
+    const size_t interiorBegin = std::max(xBegin, size_t{1});
+    const size_t interiorEnd = std::min(xEnd, nx - 1);
+    if (xBegin == 0) {
+        result[0] = apply(0, 0, nx > 1 ? 1 : 0);
+    }
+    #pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t x = interiorBegin; x < interiorEnd; ++x) {
+        result[x - xBegin] = apply(x, x - 1, x + 1);
+    }
+    if (xEnd == nx && nx > 1) {
+        result[nx - 1 - xBegin] = apply(nx - 1, nx - 2, nx - 1);
+    }
+}
+
+// Compute a tile's chemical potential, including a one-cell halo, directly from
+// the immutable old concentration. Then update just the tile's owned cells.
+// Potentials are thread-private, so tiles need no intermediate global barrier.
+void advanceTile(const double* __restrict__ cold, double* __restrict__ cnew,
+                 double* __restrict__ mu,
+                 const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
+                 const size_t xBegin, const size_t xEnd,
+                 const size_t yBegin, const size_t yEnd,
+                 const size_t zBegin, const size_t zEnd,
+                 const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,
+                 const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB,
+                 const double D, const double dt) {
+    const size_t hxBegin = xBegin > 0 ? xBegin - 1 : 0;
+    const size_t hxEnd = xEnd < nx ? xEnd + 1 : nx;
+    const size_t hyBegin = yBegin > 0 ? yBegin - 1 : 0;
+    const size_t hyEnd = yEnd < ny ? yEnd + 1 : ny;
+    const size_t hzBegin = zBegin > 0 ? zBegin - 1 : 0;
+    const size_t hzEnd = zEnd < nz ? zEnd + 1 : nz;
+    const size_t muStride = hxEnd - hxBegin;
+    const size_t muPlane = muStride * (hyEnd - hyBegin);
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const double dx2 = dx * dx;
+    const double dy2 = dy * dy;
+    const double dz2 = dz * dz;
+
+    for (size_t z = hzBegin; z < hzEnd; ++z) {
+        for (size_t y = hyBegin; y < hyEnd; ++y) {
+            const double* row = cold + z * plane + y * nx;
+            double* potentialRow = mu + (z - hzBegin) * muPlane + (y - hyBegin) * muStride;
+            stencilRow(row, row - (y > 0 ? nx : 0), row + (y + 1 < ny ? nx : 0),
+                       row - (z > 0 ? plane : 0), row + (z + 1 < nz ? plane : 0),
+                       potentialRow, hxBegin, hxEnd, nx, 0, dx2, dy2, dz2,
+                       [=](size_t, const double cv, const double laplacian) {
+                           return 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB)
+                                  + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv - gamma * laplacian;
+                       });
         }
     }
+
+    for (size_t z = zBegin; z < zEnd; ++z) {
+        for (size_t y = yBegin; y < yEnd; ++y) {
+            const double* oldRow = cold + z * plane + y * nx;
+            const double* row = mu + (z - hzBegin) * muPlane + (y - hyBegin) * muStride;
+            double* nextRow = cnew + z * plane + y * nx + xBegin;
+            stencilRow(row, row - (y > 0 ? muStride : 0), row + (y + 1 < ny ? muStride : 0),
+                       row - (z > 0 ? muPlane : 0), row + (z + 1 < nz ? muPlane : 0),
+                       nextRow, xBegin, xEnd, nx, hxBegin, dx2, dy2, dz2,
+                       [=](const size_t x, double, const double laplacian) {
+                           return oldRow[x] + dt * D * laplacian;
+                       });
+        }
+    }
 }

+// The potential tile, its concentration data and its output fit in a private
+// CPU cache. Halos contain real neighboring cells, with clamping only at the
+// physical domain boundary.
+struct TileShape {
+    size_t x, y, z;
+};
+
+inline size_t tileCount(const size_t extent, const size_t width) noexcept {
+    return extent / width + (extent % width != 0);
+}
+
+TileShape chooseTileShape(const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {
+    TileShape shape{std::max(size_t{1}, std::min(nx, size_t{512})),
+                    std::max(size_t{1}, std::min(ny, size_t{8})),
+                    std::max(size_t{1}, std::min(nz, size_t{8}))};
+    // Keep enough independent tiles for the requested team, including thin
+    // grids. Prefer wide X rows for efficient SIMD and contiguous memory access.
+    const size_t threads = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_max_threads());
+    while (tileCount(nx, shape.x) * tileCount(ny, shape.y) * tileCount(nz, shape.z) < threads) {
+        if (shape.y >= shape.z && shape.y > 1) {
+            shape.y = (shape.y + 1) / 2;
+        } else if (shape.z > 1) {
+            shape.z = (shape.z + 1) / 2;
+        } else if (shape.x > 1) {
+            shape.x = (shape.x + 1) / 2;
+        } else {
+            break;
         }
     }
+    return shape;
 }

-void cahnHilliardUpdate(std::vector<double>& cnew, const std::vector<double>& cold,

+// Apply a stencil to part of a row. Only physical X boundaries need scalar
+// handling; both tile interiors and their halos use contiguous SIMD loads.
+template <typename CellOperation>
+void stencilRow(const double* row, const double* yn, const double* yp,
+                const double* zn, const double* zp, double* __restrict__ result,
+                const size_t xBegin, const size_t xEnd, const size_t nx,
+                const size_t fieldXBegin,
+                const double dx2, const double dy2, const double dz2,
+                CellOperation operation) {
+    const auto apply = [&](const size_t x, const size_t xn, const size_t xp) {
+        const size_t localX = x - fieldXBegin;
+        return operation(x, row[localX],
+                         computeLaplacian(row, yn, yp, zn, zp, localX,
+                                          xn - fieldXBegin, xp - fieldXBegin, dx2, dy2, dz2));
+    };
+
+    const size_t interiorBegin = std::max(xBegin, size_t{1});
+    const size_t interiorEnd = std::min(xEnd, nx - 1);
+    if (xBegin == 0) {
+        result[0] = apply(0, 0, nx > 1 ? 1 : 0);
+    }
+    #pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t x = interiorBegin; x < interiorEnd; ++x) {
+        result[x - xBegin] = apply(x, x - 1, x + 1);
+    }
+    if (xEnd == nx && nx > 1) {
+        result[nx - 1 - xBegin] = apply(nx - 1, nx - 2, nx - 1);
+    }
+}
+
+// Compute a tile's chemical potential, including a one-cell halo, directly from
+// the immutable old concentration. Then update just the tile's owned cells.
+// Potentials are thread-private, so tiles need no intermediate global barrier.
+void advanceTile(const double* __restrict__ cold, double* __restrict__ cnew,
+                 double* __restrict__ mu,
+                 const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
+                 const size_t xBegin, const size_t xEnd,
+                 const size_t yBegin, const size_t yEnd,
+                 const size_t zBegin, const size_t zEnd,
+                 const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,
+                 const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB,
+                 const double D, const double dt) {
+    const size_t hxBegin = xBegin > 0 ? xBegin - 1 : 0;
+    const size_t hxEnd = xEnd < nx ? xEnd + 1 : nx;
+    const size_t hyBegin = yBegin > 0 ? yBegin - 1 : 0;
+    const size_t hyEnd = yEnd < ny ? yEnd + 1 : ny;
+    const size_t hzBegin = zBegin > 0 ? zBegin - 1 : 0;
+    const size_t hzEnd = zEnd < nz ? zEnd + 1 : nz;
+    const size_t muStride = hxEnd - hxBegin;
+    const size_t muPlane = muStride * (hyEnd - hyBegin);
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const double dx2 = dx * dx;
+    const double dy2 = dy * dy;
+    const double dz2 = dz * dz;
+
+    for (size_t z = hzBegin; z < hzEnd; ++z) {
+        for (size_t y = hyBegin; y < hyEnd; ++y) {
+            const double* row = cold + z * plane + y * nx;
+            double* potentialRow = mu + (z - hzBegin) * muPlane + (y - hyBegin) * muStride;
+            stencilRow(row, row - (y > 0 ? nx : 0), row + (y + 1 < ny ? nx : 0),
+                       row - (z > 0 ? plane : 0), row + (z + 1 < nz ? plane : 0),
+                       potentialRow, hxBegin, hxEnd, nx, 0, dx2, dy2, dz2,
+                       [=](size_t, const double cv, const double laplacian) {
+                           return 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB)
+                                  + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv - gamma * laplacian;
+                       });
         }
     }
+
+    for (size_t z = zBegin; z < zEnd; ++z) {
+        for (size_t y = yBegin; y < yEnd; ++y) {
+            const double* oldRow = cold + z * plane + y * nx;
+            const double* row = mu + (z - hzBegin) * muPlane + (y - hyBegin) * muStride;
+            double* nextRow = cnew + z * plane + y * nx + xBegin;
+            stencilRow(row, row - (y > 0 ? muStride : 0), row + (y + 1 < ny ? muStride : 0),
+                       row - (z > 0 ? muPlane : 0), row + (z + 1 < nz ? muPlane : 0),
+                       nextRow, xBegin, xEnd, nx, hxBegin, dx2, dy2, dz2,
+                       [=](const size_t x, double, const double laplacian) {
+                           return oldRow[x] + dt * D * laplacian;
+                       });
+        }
+    }
 }

+// Row pointers keep boundary handling and index arithmetic out of the SIMD loop.
+inline double computeLaplacian(const double* row, const double* yn, const double* yp,

+// The potential tile, its concentration data and its output fit in a private
+// CPU cache. Halos contain real neighboring cells, with clamping only at the
+// physical domain boundary.
+struct TileShape {
+    size_t x, y, z;
+};
+
+inline size_t tileCount(const size_t extent, const size_t width) noexcept {
+    return extent / width + (extent % width != 0);
+}
+
+TileShape chooseTileShape(const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {
+    TileShape shape{std::max(size_t{1}, std::min(nx, size_t{512})),
+                    std::max(size_t{1}, std::min(ny, size_t{8})),
+                    std::max(size_t{1}, std::min(nz, size_t{8}))};
+    // Keep enough independent tiles for the requested team, including thin
+    // grids. Prefer wide X rows for efficient SIMD and contiguous memory access.
+    const size_t threads = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_max_threads());
+    while (tileCount(nx, shape.x) * tileCount(ny, shape.y) * tileCount(nz, shape.z) < threads) {
+        if (shape.y >= shape.z && shape.y > 1) {
+            shape.y = (shape.y + 1) / 2;
+        } else if (shape.z > 1) {
+            shape.z = (shape.z + 1) / 2;
+        } else if (shape.x > 1) {
+            shape.x = (shape.x + 1) / 2;
+        } else {
+            break;
         }
     }
+    return shape;
 }

-void cahnHilliardUpdate(std::vector<double>& cnew, const std::vector<double>& cold,

+// Apply a stencil to part of a row. Only physical X boundaries need scalar
+// handling; both tile interiors and their halos use contiguous SIMD loads.
+template <typename CellOperation>
+void stencilRow(const double* row, const double* yn, const double* yp,
+                const double* zn, const double* zp, double* __restrict__ result,
+                const size_t xBegin, const size_t xEnd, const size_t nx,
+                const size_t fieldXBegin,
+                const double dx2, const double dy2, const double dz2,
+                CellOperation operation) {
+    const auto apply = [&](const size_t x, const size_t xn, const size_t xp) {
+        const size_t localX = x - fieldXBegin;
+        return operation(x, row[localX],
+                         computeLaplacian(row, yn, yp, zn, zp, localX,
+                                          xn - fieldXBegin, xp - fieldXBegin, dx2, dy2, dz2));
+    };
+
+    const size_t interiorBegin = std::max(xBegin, size_t{1});
+    const size_t interiorEnd = std::min(xEnd, nx - 1);
+    if (xBegin == 0) {
+        result[0] = apply(0, 0, nx > 1 ? 1 : 0);
+    }
+    #pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t x = interiorBegin; x < interiorEnd; ++x) {
+        result[x - xBegin] = apply(x, x - 1, x + 1);
+    }
+    if (xEnd == nx && nx > 1) {
+        result[nx - 1 - xBegin] = apply(nx - 1, nx - 2, nx - 1);
+    }
+}
+
+// Compute a tile's chemical potential, including a one-cell halo, directly from
+// the immutable old concentration. Then update just the tile's owned cells.
+// Potentials are thread-private, so tiles need no intermediate global barrier.
+void advanceTile(const double* __restrict__ cold, double* __restrict__ cnew,
+                 double* __restrict__ mu,
+                 const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
+                 const size_t xBegin, const size_t xEnd,
+                 const size_t yBegin, const size_t yEnd,
+                 const size_t zBegin, const size_t zEnd,
+                 const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,
+                 const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB,
+                 const double D, const double dt) {
+    const size_t hxBegin = xBegin > 0 ? xBegin - 1 : 0;
+    const size_t hxEnd = xEnd < nx ? xEnd + 1 : nx;
+    const size_t hyBegin = yBegin > 0 ? yBegin - 1 : 0;
+    const size_t hyEnd = yEnd < ny ? yEnd + 1 : ny;
+    const size_t hzBegin = zBegin > 0 ? zBegin - 1 : 0;
+    const size_t hzEnd = zEnd < nz ? zEnd + 1 : nz;
+    const size_t muStride = hxEnd - hxBegin;
+    const size_t muPlane = muStride * (hyEnd - hyBegin);
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const double dx2 = dx * dx;
+    const double dy2 = dy * dy;
+    const double dz2 = dz * dz;
+
+    for (size_t z = hzBegin; z < hzEnd; ++z) {
+        for (size_t y = hyBegin; y < hyEnd; ++y) {
+            const double* row = cold + z * plane + y * nx;
+            double* potentialRow = mu + (z - hzBegin) * muPlane + (y - hyBegin) * muStride;
+            stencilRow(row, row - (y > 0 ? nx : 0), row + (y + 1 < ny ? nx : 0),
+                       row - (z > 0 ? plane : 0), row + (z + 1 < nz ? plane : 0),
+                       potentialRow, hxBegin, hxEnd, nx, 0, dx2, dy2, dz2,
+                       [=](size_t, const double cv, const double laplacian) {
+                           return 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB)
+                                  + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv - gamma * laplacian;
+                       });
         }
     }
+
+    for (size_t z = zBegin; z < zEnd; ++z) {
+        for (size_t y = yBegin; y < yEnd; ++y) {
+            const double* oldRow = cold + z * plane + y * nx;
+            const double* row = mu + (z - hzBegin) * muPlane + (y - hyBegin) * muStride;
+            double* nextRow = cnew + z * plane + y * nx + xBegin;
+            stencilRow(row, row - (y > 0 ? muStride : 0), row + (y + 1 < ny ? muStride : 0),
+                       row - (z > 0 ? muPlane : 0), row + (z + 1 < nz ? muPlane : 0),
+                       nextRow, xBegin, xEnd, nx, hxBegin, dx2, dy2, dz2,
+                       [=](const size_t x, double, const double laplacian) {
+                           return oldRow[x] + dt * D * laplacian;
+                       });
+        }
+    }
 }

    18	// Row pointers keep boundary handling and index arithmetic out of the SIMD loop.
    19	inline double computeLaplacian(const double* row, const double* yn, const double* yp,
    20	                              const double* zn, const double* zp,
    21	                              const size_t x, const size_t xn, const size_t xp,
    22	                              const double dx2, const double dy2, const double dz2) noexcept {
    23	    const double cv = row[x];
    24	    const double cxx = (row[xp] + row[xn] - 2.0 * cv) / dx2;
    25	    const double cyy = (yp[x] + yn[x] - 2.0 * cv) / dy2;
    26	    const double czz = (zp[x] + zn[x] - 2.0 * cv) / dz2;
    27	    return cxx + cyy + czz;
    28	}
    29	
    30	// The potential tile, its concentration data and its output fit in a private
    31	// CPU cache. Halos contain real neighboring cells, with clamping only at the
    32	// physical domain boundary.
    33	struct TileShape {
    34	    size_t x, y, z;
    35	};
    36	
    37	inline size_t tileCount(const size_t extent, const size_t width) noexcept {
    38	    return extent / width + (extent % width != 0);
    39	}
    40	
    41	TileShape chooseTileShape(const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {
    42	    TileShape shape{std::max(size_t{1}, std::min(nx, size_t{512})),
    43	                    std::max(size_t{1}, std::min(ny, size_t{8})),
    44	                    std::max(size_t{1}, std::min(nz, size_t{8}))};
    45	    // Keep enough independent tiles for the requested team, including thin
    46	    // grids. Prefer wide X rows for efficient SIMD and contiguous memory access.
    47	    const size_t threads = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_max_threads());
    48	    while (tileCount(nx, shape.x) * tileCount(ny, shape.y) * tileCount(nz, shape.z) < threads) {
    49	        if (shape.y >= shape.z && shape.y > 1) {
    50	            shape.y = (shape.y + 1) / 2;
    51	        } else if (shape.z > 1) {
    52	            shape.z = (shape.z + 1) / 2;
    53	        } else if (shape.x > 1) {
    54	            shape.x = (shape.x + 1) / 2;
    55	        } else {
    56	            break;
    57	        }
    58	    }
    59	    return shape;
    60	}
    61	
    62	// Apply a stencil to part of a row. Only physical X boundaries need scalar
    63	// handling; both tile interiors and their halos use contiguous SIMD loads.
    64	template <typename CellOperation>
    65	void stencilRow(const double* row, const double* yn, const double* yp,
    66	                const double* zn, const double* zp, double* __restrict__ result,
    67	                const size_t xBegin, const size_t xEnd, const size_t nx,
    68	                const size_t fieldXBegin,
    69	                const double dx2, const double dy2, const double dz2,
    70	                CellOperation operation) {
    71	    const auto apply = [&](const size_t x, const size_t xn, const size_t xp) {
    72	        const size_t localX = x - fieldXBegin;
    73	        return operation(x, row[localX],
    74	                         computeLaplacian(row, yn, yp, zn, zp, localX,
    75	                                          xn - fieldXBegin, xp - fieldXBegin, dx2, dy2, dz2));
    76	    };
    77	
    78	    const size_t interiorBegin = std::max(xBegin, size_t{1});
    79	    const size_t interiorEnd = std::min(xEnd, nx - 1);
    80	    if (xBegin == 0) {
    81	        result[0] = apply(0, 0, nx > 1 ? 1 : 0);
    82	    }
    83	    #pragma omp simd
    84	    for (size_t x = interiorBegin; x < interiorEnd; ++x) {
    85	        result[x - xBegin] = apply(x, x - 1, x + 1);
    86	    }
    87	    if (xEnd == nx && nx > 1) {
    88	        result[nx - 1 - xBegin] = apply(nx - 1, nx - 2, nx - 1);
    89	    }
    90	}
    91	
    92	// Compute a tile's chemical potential, including a one-cell halo, directly from
    93	// the immutable old concentration. Then update just the tile's owned cells.
    94	// Potentials are thread-private, so tiles need no intermediate global barrier.
    95	void advanceTile(const double* __restrict__ cold, double* __restrict__ cnew,
    96	                 double* __restrict__ mu,
    97	                 const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
    98	                 const size_t xBegin, const size_t xEnd,
    99	                 const size_t yBegin, const size_t yEnd,
   100	                 const size_t zBegin, const size_t zEnd,
   101	                 const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,
   102	                 const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB,
   103	                 const double D, const double dt) {
   104	    const size_t hxBegin = xBegin > 0 ? xBegin - 1 : 0;
   105	    const size_t hxEnd = xEnd < nx ? xEnd + 1 : nx;
   106	    const size_t hyBegin = yBegin > 0 ? yBegin - 1 : 0;
   107	    const size_t hyEnd = yEnd < ny ? yEnd + 1 : ny;
   108	    const size_t hzBegin = zBegin > 0 ? zBegin - 1 : 0;
   109	    const size_t hzEnd = zEnd < nz ? zEnd + 1 : nz;
   110	    const size_t muStride = hxEnd - hxBegin;
   111	    const size_t muPlane = muStride * (hyEnd - hyBegin);
   112	    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
   113	    const double dx2 = dx * dx;
   114	    const double dy2 = dy * dy;
   115	    const double dz2 = dz * dz;
   116	
   117	    for (size_t z = hzBegin; z < hzEnd; ++z) {
   118	        for (size_t y = hyBegin; y < hyEnd; ++y) {
   119	            const double* row = cold + z * plane + y * nx;
   120	            double* potentialRow = mu + (z - hzBegin) * muPlane + (y - hyBegin) * muStride;
   121	            stencilRow(row, row - (y > 0 ? nx : 0), row + (y + 1 < ny ? nx : 0),
   122	                       row - (z > 0 ? plane : 0), row + (z + 1 < nz ? plane : 0),
   123	                       potentialRow, hxBegin, hxEnd, nx, 0, dx2, dy2, dz2,
   124	                       [=](size_t, const double cv, const double laplacian) {
   125	                           return 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB)
   126	                                  + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv - gamma * laplacian;
   127	                       });
   128	        }
   129	    }
   130	
   131	    for (size_t z = zBegin; z < zEnd; ++z) {
   132	        for (size_t y = yBegin; y < yEnd; ++y) {
   133	            const double* oldRow = cold + z * plane + y * nx;
   134	            const double* row = mu + (z - hzBegin) * muPlane + (y - hyBegin) * muStride;
   135	            double* nextRow = cnew + z * plane + y * nx + xBegin;
   136	            stencilRow(row, row - (y > 0 ? muStride : 0), row + (y + 1 < ny ? muStride : 0),
   137	                       row - (z > 0 ? muPlane : 0), row + (z + 1 < nz ? muPlane : 0),
   138	                       nextRow, xBegin, xEnd, nx, hxBegin, dx2, dy2, dz2,
   139	                       [=](const size_t x, double, const double laplacian) {
   140	                           return oldRow[x] + dt * D * laplacian;
   141	                       });
   142	        }
   143	    }
   144	}
   145	
   146	// Called by every team member; initialization uses the same static tile
   147	// assignment as the simulation to preserve NUMA locality for both buffers.
   148	void initializeConcentration(double* c, double* cnew,
   149	                             const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
   150	                             const TileShape shape) {
   151	    const size_t vol = nx * ny * nz;
   152	    const size_t tilesX = tileCount(nx, shape.x);
   153	    const size_t tilesY = tileCount(ny, shape.y);
   154	    const size_t tilesZ = tileCount(nz, shape.z);
   155	    #pragma omp for collapse(3) schedule(static)
   156	    for (size_t bz = 0; bz < tilesZ; ++bz) {
   157	        for (size_t by = 0; by < tilesY; ++by) {
   158	            for (size_t bx = 0; bx < tilesX; ++bx) {
   159	                for (size_t z = bz * shape.z; z < std::min((bz + 1) * shape.z, nz); ++z) {
   160	                    for (size_t y = by * shape.y; y < std::min((by + 1) * shape.y, ny); ++y) {
   161	                        for (size_t x = bx * shape.x; x < std::min((bx + 1) * shape.x, nx); ++x) {
   162	                            const size_t idx = z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;
   163	                            const double pseudo = ((((idx + 1) * 1299709) % vol) / static_cast<double>(vol));
   164	                            c[idx] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;
   165	                            cnew[idx] = 0.0;
   166	                        }
   167	                    }
   168	                }
   169	            }
   170	        }
   171	    }
   172	}
   173	
   174	bool validateResult(const double* c, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {
   175	    const size_t vol = nx * ny * nz;
   176	    double minVal = c[0];
   177	    double maxVal = c[0];
   178	    int invalid = 0;
   179	    #pragma omp parallel for simd schedule(static) reduction(min:minVal) reduction(max:maxVal) reduction(|:invalid)
   180	    for (size_t idx = 0; idx < vol; ++idx) {
   181	        const double val = c[idx];
   182	        invalid |= std::isnan(val) || std::isinf(val);
   183	        minVal = std::min(minVal, val);
   184	        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, val);
   185	    }
   186	
   187	    if (invalid) {
   188	        printf("Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\n");
   189	        return false;
   190	    }
   191	
   192	    printf("Concentration range: [%.6f, %.6f]\n", minVal, maxVal);
   193	    
   194	    // Values should generally stay within reasonable bounds
   195	    if (maxVal > 10.0 || minVal < -10.0) {
   196	        printf("Validation failed: values out of expected range\n");
   197	        return false;
   198	    }
   199	    
   200	    return true;
   201	}
   202	
   203	void printUsage(const char* progName) {
   204	    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
   205	    printf("Options:\n");
   206	    printf("  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 64)\n");
   207	    printf("  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\n");
   208	    printf("  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\n");
   209	    printf("  -i <num>     Number of time steps (default: 20)\n");
   210	    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
   211	    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
   212	    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
   213	}
   214	
   215	int main(int argc, char** argv) {
   216	    size_t nx = 64;
   217	    size_t ny = 0;
   218	    size_t nz = 0;
   219	    int iterations = 20;
   220	    bool validate = false;
   221	    bool printResults = false;
   222	    
   223	    // Parse command line arguments
   224	    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
   225	        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-x") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   226	            nx = atoi(argv[++i]);
   227	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-y") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   228	            ny = atoi(argv[++i]);
   229	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-z") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   230	            nz = atoi(argv[++i]);
   231	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-i") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   232	            iterations = atoi(argv[++i]);
   233	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
   234	            validate = true;
   235	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
   236	            printResults = true;
   237	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
   238	            printUsage(argv[0]);
   239	            return 0;
   240	        } else {
   241	            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
   242	            printUsage(argv[0]);
   243	            return 1;
   244	        }
   245	    }
   246	    
   247	    if (ny == 0) ny = nx;
   248	    if (nz == 0) nz = nx;
   249	    
   250	    printf("Cahn-Hilliard Phase Separation Benchmark\n");
   251	    printf("Grid size: %zu x %zu x %zu\n", nx, ny, nz);
   252	    printf("Time steps: %d\n", iterations);
   253	    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
   254	    
   255	    // Physical parameters
   256	    const double dx = 1.0;
   257	    const double dy = 1.0;
   258	    const double dz = 1.0;
   259	    const double dt = 0.01;
   260	    const double e_AA = -(2.0 / 9.0);
   261	    const double e_BB = -(2.0 / 9.0);
   262	    const double e_AB = (2.0 / 9.0);
   263	    const double gamma = 0.5;
   264	    const double D = 1.0;
   265	    
   266	    size_t gridSize = nx * ny * nz;
   267	    
   268	    // Leave storage uninitialized so each thread first-touches its own tiles.
   269	    auto cold = std::make_unique_for_overwrite<double[]>(gridSize);
   270	    auto cnew = std::make_unique_for_overwrite<double[]>(gridSize);
   271	    const TileShape shape = chooseTileShape(nx, ny, nz);
   272	    const size_t tilesX = tileCount(nx, shape.x);
   273	    const size_t tilesY = tileCount(ny, shape.y);
   274	    const size_t tilesZ = tileCount(nz, shape.z);
   275	    const size_t potentialSize = std::min(nx, shape.x + 2) * std::min(ny, shape.y + 2) * std::min(nz, shape.z + 2);
   276	    
   277	    // Initialize concentration field
   278	    printf("Initializing concentration field...\n");
   279	    std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::time_point start;
   280	    #pragma omp parallel
   281	    {
   282	        auto potential = std::make_unique_for_overwrite<double[]>(potentialSize);
   283	        std::fill_n(potential.get(), potentialSize, 0.0);
   284	        double* current = cold.get();
   285	        double* next = cnew.get();
   286	        initializeConcentration(current, next, nx, ny, nz, shape);
   287	        #pragma omp single
   288	        {
   289	            printf("Running Cahn-Hilliard simulation...\n");
   290	            start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
   291	        }
   292	
   293	        for (int t = 0; t < iterations; ++t) {
   294	            #pragma omp for collapse(3) schedule(static)
   295	            for (size_t bz = 0; bz < tilesZ; ++bz) {
   296	                for (size_t by = 0; by < tilesY; ++by) {
   297	                    for (size_t bx = 0; bx < tilesX; ++bx) {
   298	                        advanceTile(current, next, potential.get(), nx, ny, nz,
   299	                                    bx * shape.x, std::min((bx + 1) * shape.x, nx),
   300	                                    by * shape.y, std::min((by + 1) * shape.y, ny),
   301	                                    bz * shape.z, std::min((bz + 1) * shape.z, nz),
   302	                                    dx, dy, dz, gamma, e_AA, e_BB, e_AB, D, dt);
   303	                    }
   304	                }
   305	            }
   306	
   307	            // Each thread swaps its own pointers after the update barrier. This
   308	            // needs no single region or additional synchronization.
   309	            std::swap(current, next);
   310	        }
   311	    }
   312	    if (iterations > 0 && iterations % 2 != 0) {
   313	        std::swap(cold, cnew);
   314	    }
   315	    
   316	    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
   317	    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
   318	    
   319	    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
   320	    
   321	    // Calculate performance
   322	    double cellUpdates = (double)gridSize * iterations;
   323	    double mcups = cellUpdates / std::chrono::duration<double>(end - start).count() / 1e6;
   324	    printf("Performance: %.3f MCellUpdates/s\n", mcups);
   325	    
   326	    // Print results for external validation
   327	    if (printResults) {
   328	        print_results(std::vector<double>(cold.get(), cold.get() + gridSize), "Concentration");
   329	    }
   330	    
   331	    // Validation
   332	    if (validate) {
   333	        printf("Validating result...\n");
   334	        bool valid = validateResult(cold.get(), nx, ny, nz);
   335	        
   336	        if (valid) {
   337	            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
   338	            return 0;
   339	        } else {
   340	            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
   341	            return 1;
   342	        }
   343	    }
   344	    
   345	    return 0;
   346	}
cmake_minimum_required(VERSION 3.10)
project(cahn_hilliard_benchmark)

set(CMAKE_CXX_STANDARD 20)
set(CMAKE_CXX_STANDARD_REQUIRED ON)

find_package(OpenMP REQUIRED COMPONENTS CXX)

add_executable(cahn_hilliard cahn_hilliard.cpp)

# Enable optimizations
target_compile_options(cahn_hilliard PRIVATE -O3 -march=native -Wall -Wextra)

target_link_libraries(cahn_hilliard PRIVATE OpenMP::OpenMP_CXX m)

-double computeLaplacian(const std::vector<double>& c, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,

-    const size_t yp = (y < ny - 1) ? y + 1 : y;

+// Row pointers keep boundary handling and index arithmetic out of the SIMD loop.
+inline double computeLaplacian(const double* row, const double* yn, const double* yp,
+                              const double* zn, const double* zp,
+                              const size_t x, const size_t xn, const size_t xp,
+                              const double dx2, const double dy2, const double dz2) noexcept {

     return cxx + cyy + czz;
 }
 
-// Compute chemical potential
-void computeChemicalPotential(const std::vector<double>& c, std::vector<double>& mu,
-                              const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
-                              const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,
-                              const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB) {
